# BELFORT overhaul record

This is the working record of the BELFORT overhaul. The owner asked to start by mapping the current systems and content, to document what was tested and what was changed, and to end with the biggest changes and the remaining high-value issues. Sections 1 to 5 are the map, written before any change. Sections 6 to 8 are filled in as the overhaul proceeds.

The map was made by eight engineers, one per subsystem: core, player, combat, rpg, life, world, ui and content. Every subsystem has a map; none is missing. Their structured maps are kept as JSON working copies for the testers and fixers (one file per subsystem, `audit/map/<key>.json` in the session scratchpad). Where two maps disagreed, the code was checked and section 2.6 states what it does.

Line numbers refer to the code as it was mapped (commit `fc9ffdb`). Five jobs are changing parts of it in parallel: the opening job (the start-of-game flow in `src/main.ts`, `src/story/*`, `src/audio/*`), the character-kit job (`src/rpg/townsfolk.ts`, `src/rpg/look.ts`, `src/player/hero.ts`, `herokit.ts`, `scales.ts`, `src/ui/creation.ts`, `preview.ts`), the sixth-faction job (`src/combat/*`, `src/life/*`, `src/content/*`, `src/rpg/kroniek.ts`, `src/rpg/factions.ts`), the buildings job (`src/world/buildings.ts`, `landmarks.ts`, `walls.ts`, `palette.ts`, `public/textures/*`) and the world-data job (`public/world/regions/*`, `tools/regions/*`). A Flemish language pass is also rewording strings everywhere. Expect line numbers in those files to move.

## Contents

1. Systems
2. How it fits together
3. Gameplay loops
4. Content
5. Structural problems
6. What was tested
7. What was changed
8. What remains

## 1. Systems

One subsection per subsystem. The purpose, files, state, dependencies and users are as the subsystem's engineer mapped them. "ABOUT TO CHANGE" marks files another job is rewriting now. Section 2.1 settles files that two maps both describe.

### 1.1 Core (composition root, input, assets, math, render)

Core is the game's composition root and its low-level services. src/main.ts sets the game up. It reads the URL flags, builds the WebGL renderer, shows the loading curtain and the front end, loads the world, sky, textures and models, turns the front end's result into a save, and builds every gameplay system. It then wires them together with callback bags, runs the one requestAnimationFrame loop that updates everything in a fixed order, and arbitrates input, panels, pointer lock and pause. src/core holds the input layer (keyboard, mouse and gamepad turned into actions and axes), the asset fetch helper (with the "wrapped" JSON mode for restrictive hosts) and the math helpers. src/render holds the post-processing chain (N8AO, bloom, an ACES grade with vignette and grain, SMAA), GLB loading with the procedural BoneRig, the shader cloak recolour and the packed PBR texture-array loader. main.ts also carries a lot of gameplay that belongs elsewhere: Klokkenronde rewards and share text, Kroniek milestone rewards, discovery XP, respawn, place names, the HUD prompt priority and the action bar.

**Files**

| File | Lines | Role |
|---|---|---|
| `src/main.ts` | 908 | Composition root. boot() (52-903) does loading, the front-end handover, save creation, building every system, the frame loop (547-779), interaction (781-837), respawn (845-863) and place names (866-898). ABOUT TO CHANGE: the opening job rewrites its start-of-game flow (lines \~151-183 and 338-494). |
| `src/core/input.ts` | 230 | Unified input. 16 abstract Actions, a keyboard map, mouse buttons and wheel, gamepad axes, edge-triggered buttons and triggers, pointer lock with drag-look fallback, and device detection. |
| `src/core/assets.ts` | 26 | asset(): a URL relative to document.baseURI. fetchBinary() reads a binary, or its base64 '.json' wrapper when window.BELFORT_WRAPPED is set. |
| `src/core/math.ts` | 64 | clamp, lerp, damp (frame-rate-independent smoothing), angleDelta, smoothstep, hash01, rng (mulberry32), noise1, spring. smoothstep and spring are never imported. |
| `src/render/post.ts` | 106 | Post class. EffectComposer with N8AOPass (it renders the scene), UnrealBloomPass, the final grade shader (split tone, saturation, ACES, contrast, manual sRGB, vignette, grain) and SMAA. Low quality is RenderPass plus the final pass only. |
| `src/render/models.ts` | 87 | loadModel(): a meshopt-capable GLTF load through fetchBinary. Every mesh gets cast and receive shadow and has frustum culling turned off; it returns null on failure. BoneRig drives bones in model space; only rotate() is used, by the dragon. |
| `src/render/recolor.ts` | 67 | recolor(): clones a material and patches its fragment shader to swap red cloth for a cloak colour. Shared materials go in a module-level cache. makeSword() builds the procedural fallback sword (misplaced in this file). |
| `src/render/textures.ts` | 109 | loadMaterialSet(): decodes 3 JPEG strips (albedo, normal, ORM) per set into canvas pixels, packs roughness and AO into alpha, and builds DataArrayTextures plus shared uniforms. GLSL_COMMON holds the hash, value noise and toLinear shader snippets for the ground and building shaders. |
| `src/types.d.ts` | 22 | Ambient type declaration for the untyped 'n8ao' module (N8AOPass constructor, configuration, setQualityMode, setSize). |
| `index.html` | 27 | Page shell: lang nl-BE, OG/Twitter meta (og:image is a relative path), Google Fonts (Cinzel, EB Garamond) as a render-blocking external stylesheet, /src/ui/style.css, canvas#game, div#ui, and the module script /src/main.ts. |
| `vite.config.ts` | 6 | base './' (the game works from any sub-path), target es2022, chunkSizeWarningLimit 1500. Everything ships as one \~1.4 MB JS chunk. |
| `package.json` | 23 | Dependencies three ^0.170.0 (installed 0.170.0) and n8ao ^1.9.4. Dev: typescript, vite 5.4, @types/three. Scripts: dev, build (tsc --noEmit && vite build), preview, typecheck, world, critic:shots. playwright, which critic:shots needs, is neither declared nor installed. |
| `tools/package-wrapped.mjs` | 32 | Copies dist/ to an output folder and wraps .glb and .hdr files as {b64} JSON for hosts that serve only common types. It skips every index.html and prints the file list. Must be run from the repo root after a build. |

### 1.2 Player (hero, dragon, movement, camera)

The player subsystem is everything the person at the keyboard directly controls and sees from: the rider on foot (Hero), the dragon (Dragon), the controller that owns both and their five movement modes (Player), the third-person camera (CameraRig), and the device layer that turns keyboard, mouse and gamepad into axes and actions (Input), plus small math helpers. Player.update runs the flight model (throttle, boost, hover, energy, terrain assists), on-foot movement (camera-relative walk/run/jump with step-up and wall sliding), and the scripted transitions between them (land, dismount, mount, call the dragon). It drives procedural dragon animation (no clips; bones posed in code) and clip-based hero animation (AnimationMixer with stand-in aliases). main.ts glues it together each frame and decides who gets an E press (people, things, or the dragon). Combat and the prologue reach into Player directly (lock, push, heroYaw, dead, hero.act). None of the runtime state is saved. The game always starts flying over the meadows east of Diest.

**Files**

| File | Lines | Role |
|---|---|---|
| `src/player/player.ts` | 527 | Player controller: owns Dragon and Hero, the 5-mode state machine (fly, landing, walk, mounting, calling), the flight model, on-foot physics, transitions, camera focus point, and the debug/teleport placements used by respawn and the prologue |
| `src/player/dragon.ts` | 328 | Dragon visuals: a primitive stand-in mesh built in code (lines 76-214), plus procedural animation (wingbeat phase, fold, neck lead, tail lag, legs) that also poses the rigged dragon.glb through BoneRig (poseRig 237-270). No clips. Also the unused roar() |
| `src/player/hero.ts` | 348 | Rider visuals: primitive stand-in figure, clip playback for rider.glb (useModel) or the townsfolk kit (useFolk), stand-in clip aliases, one-shot acts (attack/roll/hit/death/cast/land), locomotion clip choice, cloak wind, sword visibility, riding pose. ABOUT TO CHANGE (character kit job) |
| `src/player/herokit.ts` | 87 | Builds the hero from the townsfolk kit (dressHero), borrows missing clips from rider.glb (borrowClips), darkens boots (darkenLeather, also used by ui/preview.ts), and fakes a GLTF stand-in person for other systems (kitStandIn). ABOUT TO CHANGE (character kit job) |
| `src/player/scales.ts` | 59 | The 7 dragon scale colours offered at creation (SCALES) and a shader patch that hue-shifts the dragon model's body and membrane materials (tintDragon). ABOUT TO CHANGE (character kit job) |
| `src/core/input.ts` | 230 | Unified input: KEYMAP of keys to 16 abstract actions, mouse buttons and wheel, pointer lock with drag-to-look fallback, gamepad polling with deadzone, edge detection (pressed/consume/peek), held(), and device detection (kbm or pad) |
| `src/core/math.ts` | 64 | Shared helpers: clamp, lerp, damp (frame-rate independent smoothing), angleDelta, smoothstep (unused export), hash01, rng (mulberry32), noise1 (camera shake), spring (unused export) |
| `src/camera/rig.ts` | 129 | CameraRig: mouse and right-stick aim, pad auto-follow in flight, focus smoothing with lead, distance by mode and speed, over-the-shoulder offset on foot, heightfield collision pull-in, trauma shake, bank roll, speed FOV |
| `src/main.ts` | 908 | Not in the subsystem but its glue: builds Input/CameraRig/Player (67, 128, 136-146), dresses the hero (172-182), spawn (419-421), pointer-lock pause (496-507), the frame loop order (547-779), E routing talkOrPickUp vs Player (589-591, 781-837), prompts (727-739), action-bar key labels (742-756), respawn (845-863). ABOUT TO CHANGE: start-of-game flow (opening job) |

### 1.3 Combat

Combat is all the fighting in BELFORT. On foot that is a three-hit sword combo with target assist and lunge, a dodge roll with invulnerable frames, two skills per origin, and salves. From the air it is the dragon's fireballs. Enemies come from data-driven camps that spawn and despawn around the player and use an attack-token AI: idle/wander, chase, circle, windup (the tell), strike, recover, stagger, leash back home, dead. The subsystem also covers damage and crits, hit-stop and camera trauma requests, loot drops and pickup, XP and kill reporting to the quest engine, player death (after 3.4 s it hands off to main.ts, which does the respawn), world-space HP and boss bars, map marks for camps, and a pooled effects layer (particles, slash arcs, rings, flashes, scorch marks, floating HTML text). All three files are inside the sixth-faction job's scope (src/combat/\*), so they are about to change in another worktree. The character-kit job's townsfolk.ts is also being rebuilt, and enemies.ts depends on it.

**Files**

| File | Lines | Role |
|---|---|---|
| `src/combat/combat.ts` | 1187 | The whole Combat class (ABOUT TO CHANGE: sixth-faction job). It holds the camp manager (214-265), the per-frame update (268-349), on-foot moves, combo, roll and skills (352-576), dragon breath (579-623), projectiles and explosion (626-696), damage, stagger, kill, XP and drops (699-761), loot meshes, pickup and E-loot (763-860), enemy AI think/strike/move (863-1143), player hurt and death (1071-1108), DOM HP/boss/loot bars (1146-1179) and camp map marks (1182-1186). It also defines the Enemy class (35-77), CampState, Projectile, Loot, the AttackDef table COMBO/HEAVY (120-125) and the constants SPAWN_R/DESPAWN_R/MAX_TOKENS (127-129). |
| `src/combat/enemies.ts` | 446 | Enemy data and bodies (ABOUT TO CHANGE: sixth-faction job; also depends on the townsfolk kit being rebuilt by the character-kit job). Holds the TYPES table of 5 enemy archetypes (38-62), the Body interface (65-77), HumanBody (79-222: kit or rider skinned figure, its windup/strike/hit/death clip logic), makeMusket (224-236), HoundBody (239-375: procedural Kludde dog), the shared glowTexture (378-393), WispBody (395-430) and the makeBody factory (432-446). Elite captains are built here from a hard-coded kit spec. |
| `src/combat/fx.ts` | 274 | Combat effects (ABOUT TO CHANGE: sixth-faction job). Particles is a 1400-slot ring-buffer point system with a custom shader (22-113). Fx adds slash arc, ring, flash sprite, scorch decal (last 40 kept) and sparks (124-225). Floaters is HTML floating text for damage, heals, XP, coins, notes and barks, max 60 (228-274). main.ts, life.ts and the crowd also use it. |

**State it owns, and where it is saved**

- Combat.camps: CampState[] {def, enemies, spawned, clearedAt (sentinel -1e9)} at combat.ts:79-84 and 137. In memory only; NOT saved, so every camp is fresh after a reload.
- Combat.enemies, projectiles, loot arrays (combat.ts:138-140). NOT saved: gear lying on the ground is lost on reload or after 300 s.
- Combat.campTimer (0.5 s spawn check) and Combat.time (hit-stop-scaled clock, which pauses while a UI is open because dt = 0) at combat.ts:141-142.
- Player move state: atk {def, t, done, queued, skill}, combo index, comboT, roll {t, dir}, invuln, hitstop, timeScale (combat.ts:145-151). Not saved.
- Meters: resonance 0..100 and fire 0..100 (dragon breath tank), fireCd, fireIdle, cds {skill, skill2, potion}, heal (pending potion pool), buff (Strijdkreet timer), critsLeft (Rookbom), lastFight, deadT (-1 = alive) at combat.ts:152-161. Not saved; resonance, buff, critsLeft and cds also survive reset().
- Public flags set from outside: kit (main.ts:364), quiet (main.ts:470/486), dmgTakenMul (life.ts:569 every frame), inCombat (computed each frame), tally {hits, rolls, skills, potions, breaths, telegraphs} (session only; the tutorial snapshots it), bossTarget (combat.ts:162-172).
- Per Enemy (combat.ts:35-77): pos, vel, yaw, state, t, cd, hp/maxHp, token, aggro, poiseHits, stun, blind, flash, telegraph, deadT, wanderT, wanderTo, circleDir, bar/barFill DOM, aimAt, plus hitThisSwing and lastHurt (both write-only and dead). Not saved.
- DOM owned: .ebars container (enemy bars and loot labels), .bossbar, and the Floaters .floaters layer (fx.ts:229), all appended to uiRoot.
- Three.js resources: fireTex canvas texture, coinGeo/coinMat/bottleMat/beamGeo (combat.ts:176-180); in Fx: particle buffers (1400), flashes[], scorches[] (≤40, kept all session), arc/ring/disc geometry, flashTex; module singleton glowTex (enemies.ts:378).
- Written into the save via QuestEngine: quests.s.hero.hp (sheet.h.hp, changed every hit and regen tick, saved only when something else saves); quests.s.florins (+coins in pickUp, saved at once at combat.ts:841; the value is in stuivers despite the field name); quests.s.inv.salve (drink at 568/575 and pickUp at 834/841, saved at once); quests.s.hero.bag (gear pickUp at 838, saved); hero xp/level through quests.addXp (dirty flag, saved by quests.update); s.kills and quest kill counts through quests.onKill (dirty).
- Written into Player: player.dead (combat.ts:290, 1090), player.lock (375, 397), player.heroYaw (365, 435), player position through player.push (374, 401); player.hero animations through act() and setArmed().

**Depends on**

- rpg/quests QuestEngine: .sheet, .s (inv.salve, florins, hero.bag), .test(cond) for CampDef.if, .fill() for barks, .addXp(), .onKill(type, campId, x, z, elite), .save()
- rpg/character: Sheet.derived() (maxHp, dmg, crit, armor, power, fire), Sheet.h (hp, level), Sheet.origin.skill/skill2 (id, cost, cooldown, level, name), rollGear(), RARITY, gearLines(), type Gear

**Entry points (where it is called from)**

- Boot: main.ts:357-363 `new Combat(peopleModel, world.collision, player, quests, sound, uiRoot, {trauma, hurt, died, levelUp, loot})`. trauma goes to rig.addTrauma, hurt to frame.hurt (vignette), died to respawn() at main.ts:846, levelUp is a no-op (main.ts:361), loot goes to lootNotice at main.ts:190-194.
- Boot: main.ts:364 `combat.kit = townsfolk` (must happen before the first camp spawns); main.ts:365 `combat.setCamps(diest.camps)`; main.ts:366 `scene.add(combat.group)`.
- Boot: main.ts:345-351 monkey-patches quests.onKill so that every kill Combat reports also unlocks Kroniek `wezen:<type>`, or `wezen:roelof` for an elite at camp 'galgeberg'.
- Every frame: main.ts:582 `combat.update(blocked ? 0 : dt, input, rig.yaw, rig.camera, canAct)`, where canAct = !blocked && !prologue.active && 250 ms since a UI closed. main.ts:583 then multiplies the player's dt by `combat.timeScale` (hit-stop). combat.update runs before player.update, so it consumes 'jump' for the roll first.
- Every frame (read only): main.ts:614 `st-combat` CSS class from combat.inCombat; main.ts:632-641 tutorial context reads inCombat and tally; main.ts:739 the 'Roep draak' prompt is hidden while inCombat; main.ts:745-757 action-bar slots read combat.fire, cds.potion/skill/skill2 and resonance, and inCombat for the roll slot.
- Every 0.5 s: main.ts:718 builds map marks from combat.campMarks().
- On E near gear: main.ts:786 nearby() calls combat.nearestLoot(x,z); main.ts:734 shows the 'Raap op' prompt; talkOrPickUp calls loot.take(), which runs Combat.pickUp.
- On death: Combat.update (286-293) calls ev.died() after 3.4 s, which runs main.ts:846-864 respawn(): nearest of two hard-coded towns, 10 % fee, place on foot, hero.revive(), then main.ts:861 combat.reset().
- Prologue: main.ts:486 sets combat.quiet = true (no camp spawns) and main.ts:470 sets it back to false in finish(). main.ts:464-466 prologue blast() uses combat.fx.flash/ring/scorch. src/story/prologue.ts:16,253,278 imports TYPES and makeBody to build its own wisps and Kludde.
- Level-up: main.ts:214 (the quests xp callback) draws combat.fx.ring at the player.
- Crowd barks: main.ts:367 passes `(text, at) => combat.floaters.add(text, at, 'bark', 3.2, 0.35)` to Crowd.
- Town life every frame: life.ts:569 writes combat.dmgTakenMul from blessings; life.ts:576 reads combat.inCombat to auto-eat below 50 % HP; life.ts:548 street-seller barks go through combat.floaters.add.
- Town life on talk: life.ts:848 (todo pointer), life.ts:974 (soldier 'camps' special), life.ts:1120 (gate guide) read combat.campMarks().

### 1.4 RPG rules (save, character sheet, quests, Kroniek, factions)

The rules layer under the 3D game. It holds the player's whole saved state and the character sheet: origins, backgrounds, levels, gear and loot. It runs the data-driven quest engine, which tests conditions, applies effects, tracks objectives and pays rewards, by interpreting the pure-data content in src/content/diest.ts. It places the named characters and the quest pickups in the world and puts the !/? markers over them. It holds the Kroniek (the lore codex, with history / folk tale / invented labels), the readable objects in the world that unlock it, and the period glossary. It also defines the five factions and their standing tiers, and works out how a saved hero is dressed from the townsfolk kit. Two other modules carry much of the actual behaviour: src/ui/rpgui.ts is the dialogue interpreter and the journal, and src/life/\* turns standing into prices, makes notice-board jobs and writes conversations on the fly. Nearly every other subsystem reads and writes the save object directly through QuestEngine.s.

**Files**

| File | Lines | Role |
|---|---|---|
| `src/rpg/state.ts` | 175 | Save schema (SaveData v2, HeroData, QuestState, LifeSave), freshSave/freshLife defaults, localStorage load (shallow merge), write and clear under the key 'belfort-save-v2'. |
| `src/rpg/character.ts` | 371 | Character sheet: 3 origins with 2 skills each, 5 backgrounds with one perk each, 4 stats, XP curve and level cap 20, 14 gear bases, 7 suffixes, 12 rare names, 8 unique pieces, rollGear/uniqueGear/gearValue/gearLines, and the Sheet class (derived stats, gainXp, equip, better). |
| `src/rpg/quests.ts` | 411 | QuestEngine: condition test, effect apply, start/setStage/markDone/complete, per-frame reach checks and save flush, onKill, pickups, target/activeList/nearestGiver/marker for the HUD, conversationFor, the text fill ({name},{dragon},{m\|f}). |
| `src/rpg/types.ts` | 133 | Content data model: Cond, Effect, Choice, Line, NpcDef, Objective (talk/reach/kill/collect/flag), Stage, QuestDef, ItemDef, CampDef, Content. |
| `src/rpg/actors.ts` | 219 | Places the named NPCs (kit figure, else rider clone, else capsule) on open ground. Draws the !/? sprites, plays the idle/talk blend, turns NPCs to face the player, writes engine.npcPos, and spawns the glowing quest pickups and finds the nearest one. |
| `src/rpg/kroniek.ts` | 249 | Kroniek entries (29 places from world/lore.ts plus 35 written here), READABLES positions, the Kroniek class (unlock/has/count/milestone/tip), GLOSSARY and gloss(). ABOUT TO CHANGE: the sixth-faction job rewrites this file. |
| `src/rpg/readables.ts` | 171 | Builds the readable props (board, stone, pillory, cannon) on open ground, facing away from walls, with the scroll mark and glint over unread ones. Has nearest(), signs() and unread(). |
| `src/rpg/factions.ts` | 60 | FactionId (gilden/kerk/kroon/oranje/vrije), FACTIONS text and colours, RIVALS pairs, TIERS (0/10/25/50/90), tierOf(), RENOWN_TO_FACTION. ABOUT TO CHANGE: the sixth-faction job. |
| `src/rpg/look.ts` | 162 | Hero looks: name tables for builds, hair, beards, hair colours, cloaks and face presets, plus hero and dragon names. heroOptions(kit), STOPGAP outfits per class and sex, heroSpec(kit, hero) to turn a saved hero into a kit spec, hat helpers, cloakPiece, HERO_CLIPS. ABOUT TO CHANGE: the character-kit job. |
| `src/rpg/money.ts` | 5 | stuivers(n) formatter, used by the engine banner, journal, HUD and life. Not in the assigned list, but in this folder and imported by quests.ts. |
| `src/content/diest.ts` | 595 | COMPANION (not in the subsystem): the content the engine interprets (10 items, 7 camps, 11 NPCs, 10 quests, 100 dialogue lines). ABOUT TO CHANGE: the sixth-faction job lists src/content/\*. |
| `src/ui/rpgui.ts` | 192 | COMPANION: DialogueUI, the actual dialogue-tree interpreter (shows a line, applies its effects, filters choices by Cond), and JournalUI (quest log, track toggle, purse/renown/inventory). |
| `src/ui/kroniekui.ts` | 122 | COMPANION: Kroniek panel (K), reading card, bookTabs, dead KRONIEK_TOTAL. |
| `src/life/standing.ts` | 104 | COMPANION: what standing does in play. Points, tiers, price multipliers, rival penalty, spend-to-points, renown-to-faction, the character-panel bars. ABOUT TO CHANGE: src/life/\* is in the sixth-faction job. |
| `src/life/talk.ts` | 103 | COMPANION: writes generated dialogue lines ('life:N') into q.content.dialogue and registers speakers in q.npcs. Uses {call} effects as callbacks. ABOUT TO CHANGE (src/life/\*). |
| `src/life/board.ts` | 213 | COMPANION: notice-board jobs built as QuestDefs from the id 'job\_&lt;yyyymmdd&gt;\_&lt;slot&gt;', pushed in with engine.addQuest/addItem, with level and pin kept in save flags. ABOUT TO CHANGE (src/life/\*). |

**State it owns, and where it is saved**

- SAVE (localStorage 'belfort-save-v2', state.ts:112; written by writeSave from quests.update when dirty (quests.ts:282-285), quests.take (340), quests.save() (\~25 call sites in main/combat/life/gamehud/rpgui/tutorial/hud/painting), Kroniek.unlock (kroniek.ts:193) and the new game (main.ts:164/169). No save on pagehide/visibilitychange.)
- save.v: always 2. loadSave returns a fresh save if it differs (state.ts:153); there is no migration.
- save.created: false until creation. Written main.ts:161/167, read main.ts:105/158/165. hasSave() (state.ts:139) is unused.
- save.hero.name/origin/cloak/dragon/sex/build/face/skin/hair/hairColor/beard/hat/background/faction/scales: set by creation (main.ts:162). cloak is re-dyed by life useGood (life.ts:1311) and CharacterPanel. Read by Sheet, look.heroSpec, herokit, prologue, fill(), banners. Merged shallowly over defaults (state.ts:155).
- save.hero.level/xp: Sheet.gainXp (character.ts:338), via QuestEngine.addXp.
- save.hero.hp: combat damage, life regen and buffs (life.ts:574, 1028, 1299, 1380, 1384), Sheet.gainXp/equip, clamp at boot (main.ts:227). Creation writes 99999 (main.ts:162).
- save.hero.gear (Partial&lt;Record&lt;'wapen'|'harnas'|'talisman', Gear&gt;&gt;): Sheet.equip, starting weapon main.ts:231.
- save.hero.bag (Gear[]): QuestEngine.giveGear, combat loot (combat.ts:838), sell/buy in gamehud.
- save.quests: Record&lt;questId, QuestState{status active|done|failed, stage, done[], counts{}, log[], outcome?, started}&gt;. Written only by QuestEngine. Read by board, life, crowd, journal, main. Holds content quests, walk_diest and every job\_\* ever taken (never pruned).
- save.flags: Record&lt;string, string|number|boolean&gt;, a grab-bag. Content flags: bells_ring, seals_reported/begijnhof/warned, toll_paid/bribe/seized. Tutorial: tut\_&lt;step&gt;. main.ts: prologue, dv_day/dv_row/dv_time/dv_rounds/dv_best/dv_best_day. board.ts:186-187: job\_&lt;id&gt;\_lv and job\_&lt;id&gt;\_pin (a JSON string). Content flag reached_leuven is read but never set.
- save.inv: Record&lt;itemId, count&gt;. Default {salve: 3}. give/take effects, combat potions/loot, life goods and food, gamehud salve purchase.
- save.florins: the purse, in stuivers despite the name. Default 12. Engine effects/rewards, combat, life, gamehud, respawn, Kroniek milestone, daily ring.
- save.renown: Record&lt;town, n&gt;, per-place renown. Engine renown effect and quest rewards. Shown in the journal header. Migrated once into standing (standing.ts:26-32).
- save.tracked: quest id or null. QuestEngine.start/complete, journal track button.
- save.taken: pickup keys 'quest:objective:index'. QuestEngine.take, activePickups, target. Never pruned.
- save.discovered: string[]. DEAD, never written or read (discoveries come from kroniek 'plaats:\*').
- save.counter: quest start sequence (quests.ts:182).
- save.kills: write-only counter (quests.ts:290).
- save.kroniek: string[] of unlocked entry ids. Kroniek.unlock.
- save.standing: Record&lt;FactionId, points&gt;. Set by creation (main.ts:163) and Standing.add (life/standing.ts:65); clamped at &gt;= 0.
- save.life (LifeSave, state.ts:80-106; every field defaulted by freshLife). met: service ids talked to. spots: '&lt;kind&gt;:&lt;index&gt;' keys of street spots used (the index comes from generation order, life.ts:433). spotDay: once-a-day uses. topics: guide topics asked. buffs: id -&gt; seconds, plus a stray 'etenRate' number in the same map (life.ts:1349). boardDay: DEAD. boardTaken: write-only. reads: '&lt;jobId&gt;:&lt;entry&gt;'. spent: stuivers per faction not yet turned into points. migrated: renown carried over. walk: write-only. dice [wins, games]: write-only. cocks: weathercocks taken.
- NOT SAVED: player position and mode (always spawn at 1150,95,380, main.ts:420), current dialogue, the dynamic quest/item maps (rebuilt at boot), npcPos.
- In memory, QuestEngine (quests.ts:33-40): s (the SaveData), quests Map (content plus addQuest), items Map (content plus addItem), npcs Map (content plus Talk speakers with talk: []), npcPos Map (written by Actors and Life), dirty flag, sheet.
- In memory, Kroniek: ENTRIES array and BY_ID map (module-level, kroniek.ts:131-132), which addEntries mutates. The instance's store is save.kroniek.
- In memory, Actors: npcs[] (def, root, mixer, idle/talk actions, marker sprite, shown glyph, x, z, yaw, top), the pickups Map, time.
- In memory, Readables: items[] (def, adjusted x/z, mark sprite, glint mesh), time.
- In memory, character.ts: module-level uidCounter (Date.now()%100000).
- Shared mutable content: diest.dialogue receives and loses 'life:N' lines from Talk (talk.ts:61, 92). diest.npcs[].role gets ' · &lt;faction&gt;' appended (life.ts:202-205).

**Depends on**

- world: LORE (world/lore.ts) for place entries (kroniek.ts:8, 43-50); WorldCollision.heightAt/heightAround (actors.ts:52-60, 132; readables.ts:113, 129-136)
- render: recolor (actors.ts:8) for the rider-clone fallback
- townsfolk kit (src/rpg/townsfolk.ts, character-kit job): TownsfolkKit.buildSpec/hasBody/fits/hasClip and m.hero; FolkSpec (actors.ts:83, look.ts:71-157)
- three.js: SkeletonUtils.clone, AnimationMixer, Sprite and CanvasTexture markers
- content: the diest Content object (quests, npcs, dialogue, items, camps), passed into the QuestEngine constructor
- life (inbound through QuestEvents): call/renown/completed callbacks in main.ts:222-224 go to Life.call/renown/completed

**Used by**

- main.ts: QuestEngine (start, update, target, activeList, nearestGiver, marker, npcPos, progress, fill, conversationFor, take, item, addXp, giveGear, save, onKill wrapped, s.\*), Actors, Readables, Kroniek/READABLES/ENTRIES, loadSave/freshSave/writeSave, RARITY/rollGear/uniqueGear, HERO_CLIPS, stuivers
- ui/rpgui.ts: DialogueUI (q.content.dialogue, q.apply, q.test, q.fill, q.npcs, q.s.hero.name), JournalUI (q.allQuests, q.quest, q.stageOf, q.progress, q.item, q.npcs, q.s, q.save), gloss, stuivers

### 1.5 Town life (crowd, people, shops, board, standing)

The living town of Diest (and a little of Scherpenheuvel). It does two things. First, it fills the streets: a recycled crowd of 36 walkers on the real street network, gate guards on posts, 20 merchants, guides and gatekeepers at stalls and doors, 8 market-day hawkers, and up to 400 generated street spots (house signs, doors, wells, shrines). Second, it gives the player something to do within a few steps: talk to any walker (each role offers its own service), trade (goods and rolled gear, priced and unlocked by standing), ask the guides (crier, notary, pilgrim guide, 7 gatekeepers, the inn with tales and dice), take daily jobs from the notice board, follow the 8-stop town walk, take the 12 golden weathercocks in flight, and get a map pin ("pointer") to the nearest thing not yet done. Standing with the factions (src/life/standing.ts) turns quests, jobs, purchases and small acts into points and tiers. Those tiers drive prices, locked wares, gear quality, greetings, reactive lines and the bars in the character panel. Nearly all of it is hard-wired to Diest. Note: the sixth-faction job lists src/life/\* and src/content/\*, and the character-kit job lists src/rpg/townsfolk.ts, so both are about to change.

### 1.6 World (land, buildings, collision, sky, rings, lore)

The "world" subsystem builds and lights the playable land: flat terrain (y = 0 everywhere), land cover, water, roads, about 2,500 period buildings with shader-drawn windows, the town wall, the hand-made landmarks and about 50k impostor trees. It does this once at load, from public/world/diest_1650.json (Diest and the Demer valley, local metres, origin at the Grote Markt). It owns the height-field collision that walking, flight clearance, the camera, the crowd, combat and life all query. It owns the sky: 4 photographed presets with IBL, a directional sun with a fitted shadow map, fog, and the colour-grade parameters handed to render/post. It also owns the ring courses (the first flight and the daily Klokkenronde) and the 29 discovery cards (lore.ts), which also feed the Kroniek's Plaatsen section. Behind it sits an offline Python/Node pipeline: OSM to world JSON, then Ferraris to 1560/1650, plus textures, skies and the newer 29-town regions/overworld build. The game does not load the regions output yet.

**Files**

| File | Lines | Role |
|---|---|---|
| `src/world/world.ts` | 478 | World class: build() orchestrates ground/land/water/roads/buildings/trees/discoveries, holds collision, footprints, discoveries, shared uniforms, distance culling; ribbon/alongLine helpers |
| `src/world/types.ts` | 61 | WorldData JSON schema (BuildingData, RoadData, WaterLineData, LandData, PoiData, walls) |
| `src/world/geom.ts` | 112 | GeomBuilder (non-indexed tri soup with colour, aWall, aMat attributes), triangulate (ShapeUtils), signedArea, clipHalf |
| `src/world/footprint.ts` | 124 | analyse(): OBB, ridge direction, roof pitch/rise per building; roofHeight(); pointInRing(); toRing() |
| `src/world/plots.ts` | 344 | At load, cuts the long map blocks (Ferraris-traced rows, wide or huge modern outlines) into narrow period plots: a band of 5-7 m front houses along the street (wider and two storeys on the market and main streets, eaves-to-street more often towards the walls), lower outbuildings behind; the plots tile each block exactly (checked on a grid), so the solid outline, collision union, props and spots are unchanged. data.buildings (the HUD map) is left alone |
| `src/world/collision.ts` | 71 | WorldCollision: 32 m spatial hash of footprints + linear list of cylinder columns; heightAt/heightAround/buildingAt |
| `src/world/buildings.ts` | 436 | styleFor (wall/roof palette rules), addBuilding (walls, gable roofs, stepped gables, chimneys), building MeshStandardMaterial with onBeforeCompile shader (tiles, grime, windows, interior mapping, stained glass). ABOUT TO CHANGE (buildings job) |
| `src/world/landmarks.ts` | 279 | Hand-built landmarks: Sint-Sulpitius tower (pre-1670 stump or Mustard Pot), church towers and roof turrets, Scherpenheuvel basilica + dome + 28 stars, Holle Griet cannon, Tafelrondmotte, Root-Oak lights; adds collision columns. ABOUT TO CHANGE (buildings job) |

**State it owns, and where it is saved**

- World.group (THREE.Group of every world mesh), world.ts:46, built once, not saved
- World.collision: WorldCollision.cells Map&lt;key, Footprint[]&gt; (32 m cells) + columns[] (\~78 cylinders), collision.ts:10-12. Built at load, read-only afterwards, not saved
- World.footprints: Footprint[] (2543), world.ts:48. Only used inside the world (landmarks), not saved
- World.discoveries: Discovery[] (29) with a mutable found flag, world.ts:49. Saved indirectly as Kroniek unlocks 'plaats:&lt;key&gt;' in the save (quests.s via Kroniek); restored at main.ts:251
- World.streets[] + private streetGrid Map (64 m cells), world.ts:50-51. Built, never read (dead)
- World.uniforms {uNight, uTime}, world.ts:52. uNight set from Look.night (main.ts:133), uTime advanced in main.ts:602. Not saved
- World.demerMat / plainWater materials, world.ts:53,57. Demer emissive driven from main.ts:603
- World.oak (Root-Oak light group), world.ts:54
- World.inTown closure (wall ring point-in-polygon, else radius 680 m), world.ts:56
- World.cullables Mesh[] (\~171 building chunks + \~275 tree chunks), world.ts:58
- World.blocked Set&lt;number&gt; (4 m cells under roads/water), world.ts:60. Only needed during build but kept alive
- Module global `inTown` in buildings.ts:15 (default radius 720 m), overwritten by setTownTest from the World constructor
- Module global `M` (material-layer seeds) in landmarks.ts:60, reassigned on every buildLandmarks call
- Atmosphere: sun light, dome, night, look, current preset id, sunDir, data Map (skies.json), cache Map (bg + PMREM env per visited preset, never freed), loading guard, onChange, sky.ts:59-70. Current sky is NOT saved: every session starts at 'golden' or ?sky
- RingCourse: defs, index, time, timed, running, meshes, ev, t, rings.ts:17-26. Not saved; main.ts keeps `course` ('first'|'day'|null) and dvRow
- Saved through quests.s.flags (main.ts:296-308, 342): dv_day, dv_row, dv_time, dv_rounds, dv_best, dv_best_day (Klokkenronde) and tut_land (whether the first flight runs)
- GPU resources: build+ground texture arrays (\~8x and 6x 1024^2 RGBA x2 with mips, about 150 MB), tree atlas (\~25 MB), each visited sky bg 4096x2048 RGBA (\~32 MB) + env

**Depends on**

- three r170: ShapeUtils.triangulateShape (geom.ts:82), LatheGeometry/IcosahedronGeometry/OctahedronGeometry (landmarks), InstancedMesh + MeshDepthMaterial (trees), PMREMGenerator + RGBELoader (sky), FogExp2, onBeforeCompile chunk replacement in every material
- core/math: hash01, rng, clamp
- core/assets: asset, fetchBinary (sky.ts only; trees.ts:36 and render/textures.ts:30 re-declare their own asset())
- render/textures: MaterialSet, GLSL_COMMON, loadMaterialSet (called from main.ts)
- render/post: Post.setLook(look) consumes the Look fields shadowTint/highlightTint/saturation/exposure/night (the grade values live in sky.ts LOOKS)
- renderer settings in main.ts:57-65 (logarithmicDepthBuffer: true, PCFSoftShadowMap, NoToneMapping), camera far 16000 in camera/rig.ts:29
- Data: public/world/\*.json, public/textures/\*, public/sky/\*; offline: Python 3 + cv2/numpy/PIL/shapely/osmium, node, curl, HISTCART WMS, Overpass, ambientCG, Poly Haven

**Used by**

- main.ts: World (build, group, cull, uniforms, demerMat, oak, discoveries, data.walls.gates, inTown, collision), Atmosphere (init, setPreset, advance, update, night, look.label, onChange), RingCourse/FIRST_FLIGHT/dayCourse/dayKey/dayNumber, loadTreeAtlas, WorldData
- player/player.ts: WorldCollision.heightAt/heightAround (walking steps, roofs, flight clearance, landing)
- camera/rig.ts: WorldCollision.heightAt (camera floor and occlusion)
- combat/combat.ts: WorldCollision.heightAt/heightAround (enemy ground, projectiles, loot)
- rpg/actors.ts, rpg/readables.ts: WorldCollision.heightAt/heightAround (placement)
- rpg/crowd.ts: World.inTown, World.collision, World.data.roads (street sampling every metre), World.data.walls.gates
- life/life.ts: World.collision, World.inTown, World.data.walls.gates, World.discoveries (pointing, place lookups, the basilica key), night() via atmo.night
- ui/hud.ts: Discovery type, World.discoveries (compass, map), World.data.bounds/land/waterAreas/waterLines/roads/buildings (bakeMap at 0.25 px/m)
- rpg/kroniek.ts: LORE (29 place entries)
- story/prologue.ts: setSky -&gt; Atmosphere.setPreset
- ui/gamehud.ts Guide: a heightAt callback (main.ts:761)
- render/post.ts: Look from Atmosphere.onChange
- tools/critic/capture.cjs: window.belfort.pose and ?q=high

**Entry points (where it is called from)**

- Boot, src/main.ts:109: fetch world/${?world ?? 'diest_1650'}.json; main.ts:110 fetch textures/materials.json
- Boot, main.ts:113-117: loadMaterialSet('build'|'ground') (render/textures.ts:59), loadTreeAtlas (world/trees.ts:38)
- Boot, main.ts:120-121: new World(data,{build,ground,trees}) (world.ts:62), then world.build(setLoading) (world.ts:81), synchronous
- Boot, main.ts:122-125: new Hud(uiRoot, world), hud.initMarks() (ui/hud.ts:83, compass marks for major discoveries), scene.add(world.group)
- Boot, main.ts:128-135: new Atmosphere(scene, renderer, 4096|2048|0 by quality) (sky.ts:72); atmo.onChange -&gt; post.setLook + world.uniforms.uNight; await atmo.init(?sky ?? 'golden') (sky.ts:125)
- Boot, collision handed out: CameraRig main.ts:129, Player :137, Readables :252, Actors :353, Combat :357; whole World to Crowd and to Life (main.ts:377, life.ts)
- Boot, main.ts:251: discoveries[].found restored from kroniek.has('plaats:'+key)
- Boot, main.ts:264-343: new RingCourse, startDayCourse (:275), startFirstFlight (:334); the choice at :341-343 is part of the start-of-game flow in main.ts (ABOUT TO CHANGE, opening job)
- Prologue (src/story/prologue.ts:176, 401, 478) -&gt; setSky -&gt; atmo.setPreset (main.ts:447)
- Every frame, main.ts:555-557: key T ('time') -&gt; atmo.advance() -&gt; setPreset; hud.flash after 400 ms
- Every frame, main.ts:600-606: atmo.update(camPos, focus, camY); world.cull(camPos); world.uniforms.uTime += dt; Demer emissive pulse; Root-Oak motes bob
- Every frame, main.ts:620-626: rings.update(sdt, player.pos, flying) + course bookkeeping + the .dv Klokkenronde strip; :669 tracker entry
- Every frame, main.ts:698-708: discovery radius test -&gt; kroniek.unlock, hud.discover, chime, +15 XP
- Every frame, main.ts:743/867-891: placeName() for the location line (uses discoveries, walls.gates, inTown)
- Every frame, many times: collision.heightAt/heightAround from player.ts (96-481), rig.ts:94-100, combat.ts (248-1139), crowd.ts (79-384), actors.ts, readables.ts, life.ts, Guide (main.ts:761)
- Key P (painting), main.ts:563: hides rings.group
- Death, main.ts:846-865 respawn(): collision search for open ground near the hard-coded Diest/Scherpenheuvel points
- Debug, main.ts:517-536 window.belfort.pose (tools/critic): setPreset + collision search; window.belfort exposes world, atmo, rings, courses
- Kroniek module load, rpg/kroniek.ts:8,43: LORE -&gt; 29 'plaats:' entries

### 1.7 Interface, audio and the prologue

Everything the player sees and hears that is not the 3D world: the front end (first-visit "what this is" screen, title, one-screen character creation with a live 3D preview), the in-game HUD (compass, tracker, discovery cards, location line, context prompt, toasts, controls card, parchment map, waypoint and light pillar, player frame and action bar, banners, tutorial hints), the panels (dialogue, journal, Kroniek and its reading card, character sheet and shop, share card, Het Schilderij painting), procedural audio (wind, flight, steps, combat, bells) plus voice-line playback, and the playable prologue (the night the bells fell silent, ending at dawn on the dragon). It is all DOM over the WebGL canvas, except the Guide's pillar and ring, the preview's own renderer and the prologue's actors. It is wired together almost entirely in src/main.ts, which owns most of the glue: the loading overlay, prompt priority, action-bar slots, tracker rows, place names, the Klokkenronde HUD and share text, the painting flow, book() and uiOpen().

**Files**

| File | Lines | Role |
|---|---|---|
| `src/ui/creation.ts` | 464 | Front end: first-visit promise screen, title, dead story slides, character creation (looks, background, class, faction, name, dragon, scales, BEGIN); runFrontEnd() resolves {continue\|new}. ABOUT TO CHANGE (character-creation job). |
| `src/ui/preview.ts` | 181 | HeroPreview: a second WebGL renderer with a turntable figure from the townsfolk kit, drag or arrow keys to turn, cloak re-dye, disposed at the end of creation. ABOUT TO CHANGE (character-creation job). |
| `src/ui/gamehud.ts` | 440 | Guide (3D pillar and ring plus a DOM waypoint clamped to the screen edge), PlayerFrame (HP, resonance or fire meter, XP, action-bar slots and icons, hurt vignette), Banner queue, CharacterPanel (sheet, bag, equip, sell, vendor shop and a legacy fallback stock), ShopView/ShopWare types, keyGlyph(). |
| `src/ui/hud.ts` | 378 | Hud: compass with N/O/Z/W, POI and quest marks, quest tracker, discovery card queue, location line, prompt, toast (flash), controls help, parchment map (bake plus per-frame draw, wheel zoom), pause overlay, dead title/loading overlay methods. |
| `src/ui/rpgui.ts` | 192 | DialogueUI (lines, choices, effects, voice hook, keys 1-9/Space/E/Enter/Esc) and JournalUI (quest list by kind and status, objectives, reward, track button, log, purse, renown, inventory). |
| `src/ui/kroniekui.ts` | 122 | KroniekUI: the K panel (4 shelves, progress, truth badges, locked hints) and the parchment reading card (leeskaart); also exports bookTabs(), the shared J/K/I tab strip, and an unused KRONIEK_TOTAL. |
| `src/ui/tutorial.ts` | 180 | Tutorial: 13 stall-based one-line hints (steer, climb, land, walk, talk, dodge, attack, salve, skill, breath, mount, speed, book), learned flags in save flags tut\_\*. |
| `src/ui/share.ts` | 125 | ShareCard (parchment card: kicker, title, big line, lines, copy text, optional image; COPY/SHARE/SAVE/CLOSE) and publicUrl(). |
| `src/ui/painting.ts` | 116 | paintPicture(): renders the current WebGL frame into a framed 1600x1060 'Flemish cabinet painting' JPEG blob (varnish, craquelure, brass plate). |
| `src/ui/style.css` | 628 | The single stylesheet for the whole game (59 KB, about 525 rule blocks, 256 class selectors), including combat (.ebar, .bossbar, .fl) and life (.lifenote, .lifebuffs, .standing, .vshop) styles; appended in chronological sections with later overrides. |
| `src/audio/sound.ts` | 242 | Sound: procedural Web Audio (no files) with wind loop, flap, step, land, takeoff, bell partial synth, chime phrases, swing, hit, kill, hurt, whoosh, fire, boom, shot, howl, coins, loot, levelUp, questDone. ABOUT TO CHANGE (opening job owns src/audio/\*). |

| `src/main.ts` | 909 | Not owned, but the integration host: builds every UI object and drives them each frame; holds UI glue (loading overlay 72-90, Klokkenronde HUD and share 268-333, book() 390-400, glossShift 402-410, uiOpen 415, prologue wiring 445-489, painting 560-577, prompts 728-740, slots 743-757, placeName/pace 867-899). The start-of-game flow (53-170, 339-495) is ABOUT TO CHANGE (opening job). |

**State it owns, and where it is saved**

- Hud (hud.ts:15-40): questTarget, trackerList, mapMarks, mapScale, cardQueue, cardTimer, toastTimer, hold, mapBase (baked map canvas cache), compassMarks. Runtime only, not saved.
- Guide (gamehud.ts:38-51): target, hush, time, lastText, said. Not saved.
- PlayerFrame (gamehud.ts:171-181): lastSig (slot markup signature). Not saved.
- Banner (gamehud.ts:239-242): queue, t. Not saved; queued banners are lost on reload.
- CharacterPanel (gamehud.ts:308-318): open, shop, vendor, extra, wares, waresLevel (legacy stock, re-rolled per level and per session). Writes q.s.florins, hero.bag, hero.gear (via sheet.equip), q.s.inv.salve, saved through q.save() at gamehud.ts:393/404/413/420/434.
- DialogueUI (rpgui.ts:13-19): open, npcId, line, onClose, onLine. Quest state changes go through q.apply and are saved by the quest engine.
- JournalUI (rpgui.ts:106-108): open, sel. Writes q.s.tracked, saved at rpgui.ts:188.
- KroniekUI (kroniekui.ts:17-23): open, reading, sel, cat. Unlocks are saved by rpg/kroniek, not here.
- ShareCard (share.ts:28-31): open, res (including an image Blob). Not saved.
- Tutorial (tutorial.ts:76-88): acc, base, dt, enabled, cur, visible, waited, shown, gap. Learned steps are saved as q.s.flags['tut\_'+id] through the save callback (tutorial.ts:101-105, 152-153, 171-172), i.e. quests.save().
- Front end and creation (creation.ts:54-167): gestured, the NewHero draft h, nameTouched, pinned, shown, kit, kitState (write-only), opts. The result is written by main.ts:159-170 (freshSave plus fe.hero, standing[faction] = FIRST_LOYALTY 12, writeSave).
- HeroPreview (preview.ts:12-28): renderer, scene, turntable, figure, mixer, kit, spec, yaw, spin, drag, height, dirty. Disposed at the end of creation.
- Prologue (prologue.ts:77-110): active, stage, objective, target, prompt, allowMount, t, queue, subLeft, speaking, said (lines and once-flags), skipHeld, skipDown, sis, sisGoal, kludde, kPos, kYaw, wisps, wispLights, tolls, tollT, fired. NOT saved: only q.s.flags.prologue = true is set by main.ts:469 and saved at main.ts:482 when it finishes.
- Sound (sound.ts:6-11): ctx, master, windGain, windFilter, noise buffer, muted (never read). Not saved.
- Voice (voice.ts:11-13): have (Set of ids from the manifest), cur, volume (never changed). Not saved.
- main.ts UI state it owns for this subsystem: toasts queue and toastAt (186-187), pendingShop (188), uiClosedAt (388), mapOpen (541), tut tracker {turned, walked, ...} (260), tutGate (261), course/dvRow (273-274), the Klokkenronde flags dv_day/dv_row/dv_time/dv_rounds/dv_best/dv_best_day saved in q.s.flags (299-310).

- rpg/kroniek.ts gloss(): emits &lt;span class="gl" data-g&gt; styled by style.css:476-480

**Entry points (where it is called from)**

- BOOT main.ts:105 runFrontEnd(uiRoot, saveSummary|null, () =&gt; sound.start(), kitP) unless ?autostart; it resolves at main.ts:153 (await front). creation.ts:461-462 picks title() for a returning player and promise(false) for a first visit.

### 1.8 Content (text and story data)

Everything the player reads in BELFORT's playable chapter 1 (Diest and Scherpenheuvel, 1650), all in Dutch: the quests and their journal entries, branching conversations with 11 quest characters, the talk of 20 named merchants, guides and gatekeepers and of the street crowd, the Kroniek codex (83 pages, each labelled Geschiedenis, Volksverhaal or Verzonnen), 29 discovery cards, 15 readables, the prologue script, tutorial hints and the interface strings. It also holds the design documents that govern the text: voice.md (writing rules), story.md (the seven-chapter story the game is now heading for) and history-check.md (fact and name verdicts). The data is static TypeScript constants, read by the quest engine (src/rpg/quests.ts), town life (src/life/\*), the crowd, the Kroniek and the UI. There are about 28,800 words of game text: 25,898 in the eight data sources (measured by script) and about 2,750 more inside code files (life.ts, character.ts, main.ts, src/ui).

**Files**

| File | Lines | Role |
|---|---|---|
| `src/content/diest.ts` | 595 | Quest content (Content object): 10 items, 7 enemy camps, 11 quest NPCs with looks and talk rules, 10 quests, 100 dialogue nodes. ABOUT TO CHANGE (sixth-faction job owns src/content/\*). |
| `src/content/diest_life.ts` | 603 | Town life data: 20 service NPCs, the notice board, faction mapping and quest standing, the wares of 10 shops, talismans and specials, 19 Kroniek PAGES, the notary's and pilgrim guide's topics, the crier's news, the town walk, gate lore, inn tales, dice, board jobs, and lines for signs, doors, shrines and wells. ABOUT TO CHANGE (sixth-faction job). |
| `src/content/diest_talk.ts` | 443 | What street people say: role labels, name pools, greetings by faction tier, where-am-I and what-to-do frames, place lines, gossip by role, reactive lines, role specials (tend, camps, bless, pilgrim, dares, tales), town view and reputation lines. ABOUT TO CHANGE (sixth-faction job). |
| `src/world/lore.ts` | 170 | 29 discovery cards (LORE), keyed by world POI names, 8 of them major (shown on the compass and the map). They are also the Kroniek's 'plaats' pages. |
| `src/rpg/kroniek.ts` | 249 | Kroniek model: truth labels, 4 categories, 35 hand-written entries plus 29 place entries built from LORE, addEntries() for PAGES, 15 READABLES, the Kroniek class (unlock, milestone every 10 entries, tip), and a 27-word GLOSSARY with the gloss() hover markup. ABOUT TO CHANGE (sixth-faction job lists kroniek.ts). |
| `src/rpg/crowd.ts` | 516 | Walker simulation. Its text is GREET (14), BELLS (6), FAMOUS (5) and FLEE (5) at lines 491-516, plus 9 inline reactive barks in line() at 474-488. |
| `src/rpg/folk.ts` | 333 | Called a 'stopgap' costume builder (dress/pickRole) for walkers when there is no kit, but LINES at 321-333 (34 short barks by role) is still the bark pool for kit walkers too (townsfolk.ts:373). |
| `src/life/lines.ts` | 89 | LinePicker (avoids repeating a line within 10 minutes), fill() for placeholders, seeded RNG, hash, today(), and the period words for direction and distance. ABOUT TO CHANGE (src/life/\* is in the sixth-faction job). |
| `src/story/prologue.ts` | 485 | The playable prologue: 16 lines in PROLOGUE_LINES (44-61), the epigraph and place card (64-65), the title card (402), objectives (221, 266, 313, 385) and the counted bell strokes (241, which are not in PROLOGUE_LINES). ABOUT TO CHANGE (opening job owns src/story/\*). |
| `src/ui/creation.ts` | 464 | Front end: the what-this-is screen, title, two dead story slides, and the character creation lore panel and labels. ABOUT TO CHANGE (character-creation job). |
| `src/ui/preview.ts` | 181 | Live 3D figure in creation. It has no player-facing text. ABOUT TO CHANGE (character-creation job). |
| `src/ui/gamehud.ts` | 440 | Guide waypoint, player frame, banners, character panel and shop panel. Interface strings, plus a hardcoded fallback 'MARIEKES WAREN' shop. |
| `src/ui/hud.ts` | 378 | Compass, discovery cards, controls help (BESTURING), map legend and credit, pause overlay. It has dead title and loading methods. |
| `src/ui/kroniekui.ts` | 122 | Kroniek panel (K), the reading card (leeskaart), book tabs, and an unused KRONIEK_TOTAL. |
| `src/ui/painting.ts` | 116 | Het Schilderij: draws the framed painting with its brass-plate text. It holds no strings of its own (the title and plate come from main.ts:569-573). |
| `src/ui/rpgui.ts` | 192 | Dialogue box and journal: section titles, reward line, track buttons, empty-state lines. |

| `src/ui/tutorial.ts` | 180 | 13 tutorial hints, each shown only after the player stalls at that step. |
| `docs/design/voice.md` | 116 | Writing rules, length limits (remark 12 words, Kroniek 60, card 2-3 sentences, hint 8) and the fixed prologue text. Partly stale against history-check.md. |
| `docs/design/story.md` | 179 | The long story (the loan, the Telster, the Klokkengieter, the Gulden Draak, the Waterwolf, the ledger) and what chapter 1 must plant. |
| `docs/research/history-check.md` | 413 | Verdicts on every name and fact (82 facts, 16 anachronisms, 46 sources). Most of its corrections are applied; several are not (see problems). |

**State it owns, and where it is saved**

- Kroniek unlocked ids: quests.s.kroniek (string[]), saved to localStorage by state.ts:163 on every unlock (kroniek.ts:193).
- Quest state, flags and inventory set by dialogue effects: quests.s.quests, quests.s.flags (bells_ring, seals_reported, seals_begijnhof, seals_warned, toll_paid, toll_bribe, toll_seized, prologue), quests.s.inv (salve, klepel, …), quests.s.renown (keys Diest, Begijnhof, Scherpenheuvel), quests.s.florins (which holds stuivers) and quests.s.tracked. All saved through quests.save().
- Tutorial: flags tut\_&lt;step&gt; in quests.s.flags (saved). Current step, wait timers and accumulators live in memory (tutorial.ts:78-88).
- Town life: quests.s.life (merchants met, buffs, standing, job pins). Saved. Owned by src/life, but written for content events.
- LinePicker.said (lines.ts:19): which lines were said in the last 10 minutes. Memory only, lost on reload.
- Crowd bark cooldowns barkCd and barkGlobal (crowd.ts:39, 64). Memory only.
- kroniek.ts module-level ENTRIES and BY_ID: mutable and grown by addEntries at import time. Not saved, rebuilt every boot.
- Prologue stage, its said set and its queue (prologue.ts:78-99). Memory only. Only flags.prologue persists.
- Creation: the NewHero object h (creation.ts:160). It becomes HeroData in the save. Pinned lore key and kit state are memory only.
- HUD: cardQueue, mapScale, compass marks (hud.ts:16-37) and the journal's sel. Memory only.

**Depends on**

- rpg/types: Content, QuestDef, Line, effects ({start, complete, stage, done, give, take, set, open:'shop', florins, renown, gear, xp, call}).
- life/types: Good, JobTemplate, Page, ServiceDef, Topic (diest_life.ts:12).
- rpg/factions: FactionId ('gilden'|'kerk'|'kroon'|'oranje'|'vrije'), used by NPC_FACTION, QUEST_STANDING, ROLE_INFO, TIER_GREET and REP.
- rpg/folk: Role (11 walker roles) for GOSSIP, ROLE_INFO, WHERE_FRAMES and LINES.
- rpg/character: gear ids in rewards (kluddevel, roelofs_slagzwaard, pelgrimsmantel, zegelring; character.ts:219-223), and ORIGINS, BACKGROUNDS and STAT_NAMES shown in creation.
- combat/enemies: TYPES ids used in camps (deserteur, musketier, broekhond, dwaallicht, kludde; enemies.ts:40-59).
- World data: POI keys must match LORE keys and PLACE_LINES keys. Gate keys (Antwerpsepoort, …) must match GATES and the reveal lists in GOODS. Readable and NPC coordinates are in world metres.
- rpg/look and the townsfolk kit: wear piece and palette ids in every `look` (diest.ts, diest_life.ts, life.ts MARKET_DAY).
- rpg/money: stuivers() formats every amount.

**Used by**

- main.ts: `diest` goes to new QuestEngine (195). ENTRIES feed the loading tips (76). Kroniek and READABLES (21, 237, 252). runFrontEnd (105). paintPicture (573). Hud.announce/discover (443, 704).
- rpg/quests.ts: content.dialogue, npcs, items, quests and camps. fill() for {name}, {dragon} and {a|b}.
- life/life.ts:28-34: imports almost every export of diest_life and diest_talk (GOODS, SERVICES, TOPICS, NEWS, GATES, WALK, GOSSIP, REACTIVE, TIER_GREET, TALES, …) and holds its own texts (MARKET_DAY 105-123, COCKS 125-135, walkQuest 1506-1517, menu labels 1103-1140).
- life/board.ts:10 (JOBS, FETCH_SPOTS, SERVICES). life/shop.ts:10 (GEAR_STOCK, SPECIALS, TALISMANS).
- rpg/townsfolk.ts:20 and 373: folk LINES become the bark pool for kit walkers. rpg/crowd.ts:11 uses dress and pickRole when there is no kit.
- rpg/readables.ts:7 (Readable). world/world.ts:13 (LORE turns into discoveries, titles and texts).
- ui/rpgui.ts and ui/kroniekui.ts: gloss() and the GLOSSARY. ui/kroniekui.ts: CATS, ENTRIES, TRUTH.

**Entry points (where it is called from)**

- Boot, at import: src/main.ts:9 imports `diest`. src/life/life.ts:44 calls addEntries(PAGES) at module load, which mutates the Kroniek's ENTRIES. src/rpg/kroniek.ts:43 builds the place entries from LORE. src/world/world.ts:13 turns LORE into discoveries.
- Boot: main.ts:73 shows the loading title and main.ts:76-86 rotates loading tips taken from the Kroniek's tijd/geschiedenis entries every 7 s. main.ts:105 runFrontEnd() shows the what-this-is screen, the title and creation (creation.ts:50).
- Boot: main.ts:195 new QuestEngine(diest, …). main.ts:237 new Kroniek(quests.s, {unlocked, milestone}). main.ts:252 new Readables(READABLES). main.ts:256 new Tutorial. main.ts:367 new Crowd. main.ts:369-375 create Guide, PlayerFrame, Banner, DialogueUI, JournalUI and CharacterPanel. main.ts:445 new Prologue.
- New game: main.ts:485-488 prologue.start(). At its end (main.ts:479-482) mens:neelke is unlocked, startFirstFlight() runs, and beginChapter() (main.ts:441-444) starts main_arrive and queues the card 'Onder je ligt Diest. De klokken zwijgen.'
- Every frame: prologue.update (main.ts:589), readables.update (619), tutorial.update (633), crowd.update with barks (642, crowd.ts:417-420), life.update (645), frame/banner/guide/hud.update (757-763), and the discovery check, which unlocks plaats:\* and calls hud.discover (main.ts:699-706).
- On E (main.ts:800-834): a readable leads to kroniek.unlock + kroniekUI.show (812); a quest NPC leads to kroniek.unlock('mens:id') + dialogue.start(conversationFor) and, after it, the shop if a line set pendingShop (815-826); anything else in town leads to life.interact (829), the conversations of walkers, services, board and spots.
- On events: a kill unlocks wezen:&lt;type&gt;, or wezen:roelof when an elite falls at camp 'galgeberg' (main.ts:347-350). bells_ring becomes true leads to tijd:klank and the 'De klokken luiden weer' banner (main.ts:684-690). Learning tutorial step 'land' unlocks tijd:draken (main.ts:258). Every 10th Kroniek entry gives a milestone banner: Marieke pays 20 stuivers plus gear (main.ts:242-248).
- On keys: J/K/I open the journal, Kroniek and character panel (main.ts:393-399). P paints the picture and shows the share card (main.ts:565-576). H toggles help (main.ts:554).

## 2. How it fits together

BELFORT is one web page. `index.html` loads `src/main.ts` as a single module. That file is the composition root: it builds every system, wires them together with callback objects, and runs the one `requestAnimationFrame` loop. There is no event bus and no system registry; systems reach each other through constructor arguments, closures in `boot()` and a few public fields. All saved state is one JSON object in `localStorage`.

### 2.1 Who owns what

Several files appear in more than one map. This record assigns each to one owner.

| File or area | Described by | Owner in this record | Why |
|---|---|---|---|
| `src/main.ts` | every map | core | It is the composition root. Each other map describes the glue it needs there. |
| `src/core/input.ts`, `src/core/math.ts` | core, player | core | Shared services. The player map describes how input moves the body. |
| `src/render/textures.ts`, `src/render/post.ts` | core, world | core | The render chain. The world map supplies the texture sets and the look values (`sky.ts` LOOKS) that `post.setLook` applies. |
| `src/rpg/crowd.ts`, `src/rpg/folk.ts` | life, content | life | They live in `src/rpg/` but are the street crowd. Content owns their text only. |
| `src/rpg/townsfolk.ts` | life, rpg, combat, player | life (the character-kit job is rebuilding it) | The kit is used by the crowd, people, named NPCs, enemies, the hero and creation. |
| `src/rpg/kroniek.ts`, `src/world/lore.ts` | rpg, content, world | rpg (code), world (cards), content (text) | The Kroniek model is rpg; the discovery cards are built into the world; the words are content. |
| `src/content/*` | content, rpg, life | content | rpg reads `diest.ts`; life reads `diest_life.ts` and `diest_talk.ts`. |
| `src/life/standing.ts`, `talk.ts`, `board.ts` | life, rpg | life | rpg lists them as companions because they change the save and feed the quest engine. |
| `src/ui/rpgui.ts`, `src/ui/kroniekui.ts` | ui, rpg, content | ui | `DialogueUI` is the actual dialogue interpreter for the quest engine. |
| `src/story/prologue.ts`, `src/audio/*` | ui, content, player | ui (the opening job is rewriting them) | The prologue is a scripted UI and control sequence; audio is presentation. |
| `src/combat/*` | combat | combat | Also used by the prologue (`TYPES`, `makeBody`) and by life (`floaters`, `campMarks`, `dmgTakenMul`). |

### 2.2 Boot sequence

Numbers in brackets are lines of `src/main.ts`.

1. `index.html` loads the Google Fonts stylesheet (render-blocking) and `style.css`, then evaluates the single module bundle. Module evaluation already runs side effects: `life.ts` adds 19 Kroniek pages with `addEntries(PAGES)`, and `kroniek.ts` builds 29 place entries from `world/lore.ts`.
2. `boot()` parses the URL flags, creates the WebGL renderer (logarithmic depth buffer; antialias only on low quality), the scene, `Input` (which attaches every keyboard, mouse and pointer-lock listener before any UI exists) and `Sound` (no AudioContext yet).
3. A loading curtain with rotating Kroniek tips goes up [72-90]. The save is peeked once [91]. The townsfolk kit (about 7.4 MB) starts downloading; the dragon model starts only after the kit has finished; `rider.glb` is fetched only if the kit lacks clips.
4. The front end starts in parallel [105]: the first-visit "wat is dit" screen or the title, then character creation. Loading continues behind it.
5. The world JSON (`public/world/diest_1650.json` by default) and `materials.json` are fetched [109-110] without checking `res.ok`. The building and ground texture strips are decoded on the main thread and the tree atlas is loaded.
6. `new World` and `world.build()` run synchronously: ground, land, water, roads, about 2,500 buildings, the wall, landmarks, about 53,000 impostor trees and the 29 discoveries. The main thread is blocked for the whole build, so the creation screen freezes if the player is still in it, and the six progress lines never paint.
7. The HUD, `Atmosphere` (sky preset, PMREM lighting, shadow map size by quality), `CameraRig`, `Post` and `Player` are created. `atmo.onChange` is wired before `atmo.init` so the first look reaches the post chain and the night uniform. The dragon model is applied when it arrives.
8. `await front` [153]. Boot waits here until the player has finished creation or chosen Verder spelen. Then the curtain fades and `sound.start()` runs (a no-op if the front end already started audio on its first gesture).
9. New game: a fresh save is built from the front-end hero (with `hp: 99999`, clamped later, and standing 12 with the chosen faction) and written to `localStorage` [159-170]. The hero is then read back from `localStorage` [173], dressed from the kit, and the dragon tinted.
10. Every gameplay system is built in one synchronous run: `QuestEngine` (which reads the save from `localStorage` a third time) [195], the starting weapon [226-234], `Kroniek`, `Readables`, `KroniekUI`, `Tutorial`, the ring course and share card, the `quests.onKill` monkey-patch [345-351], `Actors`, `Combat` [357] with its kit and camps, `Crowd`, the HUD pieces and panels, and `Life` with `life.init()` [377-386]. Construction order matters (section 5 lists the hidden dependencies).
11. The player is placed flying at (1150, 95, 380) facing the town. `input.requestLock()` [436] runs, no longer inside a user gesture. Voice lines are loaded without waiting (there are none). The prologue object is built.
12. One of three openings runs: the prologue (new game), `beginChapter()` (new game without the prologue: starts `main_arrive`), or the "VORIGE KEER" banner (continued game).
13. `window.belfort` is assigned (31 keys, used by the critic and review tools). `post.render(0)` compiles the shaders for what the spawn camera sees [902], then the frame loop starts. Anything first seen later (enemies, crowd variants, life props, the prologue set) compiles on first sight. A rejection anywhere in `boot()` replaces the UI with "Er ging iets mis" and the raw error text [906].

### 2.3 Inside one frame

`frameLoop` queues the next frame first, so an exception later in the frame does not stop the loop (but then nothing is drawn). The order is fixed:

1. Time: `dt` is the clock delta clamped to 1/20 s, so the game runs in slow motion below 20 fps. `input.poll()` reads keyboard axes, gamepad axes and pad buttons.
2. Global keys, if not paused: H help, T time of day, M map, P painting, J/K/I/Tab books. These are checked only against open panels, not against the prologue.
3. Combat runs first: `combat.update(dt, ...)` with `canAct` false during the prologue and for 250 ms after a panel closed. It consumes attack, skill, potion and, in combat, the jump press (for the roll). It sets `timeScale` (0.05 during hitstop).
4. Scaled time: `sdt = dt * combat.timeScale`, or 0 while a panel or the map is open.
5. Interaction and movement: with a panel open, interact and jump are consumed. Otherwise the prologue updates, then `talkOrPickUp()` gets the E press if something is near on foot, and only if it did not is `player.update(sdt, ...)` run (flight model, walking, transitions).
6. Camera: `rig.update(dt)` (or a tiny dt while paused).
7. World upkeep, always: `player.focus`, `atmo.update` (sky dome and shadow frustum), `world.cull`, water time, the Demer glow, the oak lights. The `#ui` state classes (panel open, flying, in combat, hurt) are set.
8. Game systems, if not paused: `quests.update` (reach objectives; it writes the save when dirty), actors, readables, rings and the Klokkenronde, the tutorial, the crowd, life, then the guidance (guide pillar, HUD quest target, tracker list, life pointer and prologue overrides), one toast every 1.9 s of wall clock, the bells event, discovery checks (Kroniek unlock, card, chime, 15 XP) and map marks every 0.5 s.
9. HUD, always: location line, the context prompt (from the same priority list `talkOrPickUp` uses), the action bar and player frame, banner, guide, wind sound, compass and HUD timers, then `post.render(dt)` and `input.endFrame()` (clears this frame's presses).
10. Adaptive resolution, every 1.5 s of clamped time: below 38 fps the pixel ratio drops by 14%, above 57 fps it rises by 7%, and every render target is reallocated.

The player, actors, readables, rings and crowd receive the hitstop-scaled `sdt`. The prologue, tutorial, camera, banners, guide, HUD, sky, water and post chain receive the real `dt`. Toast spacing, the 250 ms panel guard and many delays (`setTimeout` for the share card, the chapter card, the time-of-day label and the bell chimes) run on the wall clock and keep running while paused.

### 2.4 Where saving happens

There is one save: the `SaveData` v2 object under the `localStorage` key `belfort-save-v2` (`src/rpg/state.ts`). It holds the hero (look, origin, background, faction, level, XP, HP, gear, bag), quest states, flags (story, tutorial, Klokkenronde and notice-board pins), inventory, the purse (`florins`, which counts stuivers), per-place renown, faction standing, the tracked quest, taken pickups, Kroniek unlocks and the town-life block (`life`: people met, spots used, topics asked, buffs, weathercocks and more).

- `writeSave` is called from five places: the new-game write and the autostart default (`main.ts:164`, `169`), the quest engine's per-frame flush when its dirty flag is set (`quests.ts:284`), quest pickups (`quests.ts:340`) and `QuestEngine.save()` (`quests.ts:409`).
- `quests.save()` is called from 23 places in 9 files: `main.ts`, `combat.ts` (every coin and salve pickup), `life.ts`, `gamehud.ts` (buy, sell, equip), `rpgui.ts` (track button), `tutorial.ts`, `hud.ts`, `painting.ts` and `kroniek.ts` (every unlock).
- Many changes only set fields and wait for some later save: HP from combat and regeneration, buffs, the Klokkenronde flags, `start()` of a quest (it does not set the dirty flag). There is no save on `pagehide` or `visibilitychange`.
- Not saved at all: the player's position and mode (every load starts flying at the spawn point), the sky preset, cleared camps and loot on the ground, shop stock, prologue progress, bark and line memory.
- Reading: the save is parsed three times at boot (`main.ts:91`, `173`, `quests.ts:43`). A save whose version is not 2 is silently replaced by a fresh one; there is no migration. If storage is blocked, every write fails silently and the created hero is replaced by defaults.

### 2.5 How the front end hands over to the game

`runFrontEnd` (`src/ui/creation.ts`) is started early and returns a promise. The first pointer or key press inside it starts the AudioContext. The player sees the title (or the first-visit promise screen), then creation with a live 3D preview that waits for the kit. Pressing BEGIN (or Enter anywhere, even in a name field) resolves `{mode: 'new', hero}`; Verder spelen resolves `{mode: 'continue'}`; `?autostart` skips the front end.

Boot resumes at `await front`. The front end fades out over 900 ms and the curtain over 700 ms, but the canvas has never been drawn: it stays black while the hero is dressed, every system is built and the first frame compiles its shaders. The new hero is handed to the game through `localStorage` (written, then read back), not passed in memory. Letters typed into the name fields are still queued in `Input` as game actions and fire on the first frame, because `Input` saw every key from the start and nothing clears it at the handover. After the handover the game decides the opening from the front-end result (`fe.mode === 'new'`), not from the save, which is why a reload during the prologue skips both the prologue and the first quest.

### 2.6 Where the maps disagreed, and what the code does

| Question | Maps said | The code |
|---|---|---|
| What counts as "on foot" | player: walk, calling, mounting. combat: walk, calling. | Both are right: `Player.onFoot` includes mounting (`player.ts:524-526`); Combat defines its own local `onFoot` without it (`combat.ts:275`). There are two definitions. |
| Respawn banner text | core, player: "GESNEUVELD". combat, rpg, ui: "VERSLAGEN". | `VERSLAGEN · Je komt bij in <town>` with the surgeon's fee line (`main.ts:863`). |
| Pause overlay text | core: "GEPAUZEERD / KLIK". ui: "PAUZE · KLIK OM VERDER TE SPELEN". | "PAUZE" and "KLIK OM VERDER TE SPELEN" (`hud.ts:120`). |
| Quest objective counts | rpg: 16 talk, 5 kill, 3 collect, 2 reach, 1 flag. content: 11, 7, 4, 4, 1. | rpg is right. `diest.ts` has 27 objectives: 16 talk, 5 kill, 3 collect, 2 reach, 1 flag. |
| Kill objectives | combat: 5. content: 7. | 5 (rovers, honden, kludde, roelof, licht). |
| Quest items | rpg: 7. content: "6", listing 7. | 7 items are marked `quest: true`. |
| Words in the quest dialogue | rpg: about 2,360. content: 2,636. | 2,632 words over 137 text fields (100 lines and 37 choices). |
| Kroniek truth labels | rpg gave the PAGES split as 14, 1, 4 in the order verzonnen, geschiedenis, volksverhaal. | PAGES: 14 geschiedenis, 4 volksverhaal, 1 verzonnen. All 83 entries: 57 geschiedenis, 7 volksverhaal, 19 verzonnen. |
| Crowd barks | life: 72 (8 inline). content: 73 (9 inline). | 74: 34 role lines in `folk.ts`, 30 in the crowd's fixed lists, and 10 conditional lines inline in `crowd.ts` `line()` (474-488). |
| First-flight tutorial gates | world: "steer at ring 2". ui: "steer at ring 1, climb at ring 2". | Steer is asked once the first ring is passed (`rings.index === 1`); climb while heading for the third ring and more than 14 m below it (`main.ts:337`). |
| Where Scherpenheuvel is | core: respawn uses (-5040, 560). | Respawn uses (-5040, 560) (`main.ts:847`); the location line, the crowd and the weathercocks use (-5062, 518) (`main.ts:883`, `crowd.ts:75`, `life.ts:128`). |
| Gamepad bindings | core: 15 bindings. player: 13 buttons. | No conflict: core counts bindings (a button can have two), player counts buttons. |
| What happens to E right after a panel closes | core's frame order reads as "talkOrPickUp, else player.update". | Confirmed: within 250 ms of closing, `talkOrPickUp` is skipped but `player.update` still runs and consumes E, so the dragon is mounted or called (`main.ts:590-592`). |
| When the starting weapon is saved | core: "with the next quests save". rpg: "in memory only". | Both: it is set in memory at boot and written by the next save, which in a new game is the end of the prologue. |
| `main.ts` line numbers | — | The core and player maps were made against a copy of `main.ts` one line shorter; their line numbers after about line 52 are one (sometimes two) lower than the current 909-line file. The other maps match the current file. |

## 3. Gameplay loops

Every loop and interaction path from the eight maps, merged where two or more maps described the same thing. Nothing was dropped: when loops were merged, the steps of every source map are kept, each under its map's name. Testers fill in the Tested column (date, build, result, notes) as they walk each loop. File references in the steps are the source maps' own.

### 3.2 Frame, input, camera and pause

| # | Loop | Steps | Source maps | Tested |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 7 | One frame: update order and world upkeep | **core: One frame (exact update order, frameLoop main.ts:547-779)**<br>1. requestAnimationFrame(frameLoop) is queued first (548), so an exception later in the frame does not stop the loop.<br>2. dt = clamp(clock.getDelta(), 0, 1/20) (549): below 20 fps the game runs in slow motion. Then input.poll() (550) and sdt = dt.<br>3. If running and not paused: H, then hud.toggleHelp (553). T, then atmo.advance() and a hud.flash of the new label 400 ms later on the wall clock (554-557). M (unless a panel is open), then hud.toggleMap (558). P (unless a panel is open), then hide the signs, post.render(0), restore them, paintPicture and shareCard.show (559-576). J, K, I or Tab, then book() (577-579).<br>4. blocked = uiOpen() \|\| mapOpen. combat.update(blocked ? 0 : dt, input, rig.yaw, rig.camera, canAct) (581). canAct requires no prologue and at least 250 ms since a panel closed. Combat sets timeScale (0.05 during hitstop).<br>5. sdt = blocked ? 0 : dt \* combat.timeScale (582).<br>6. With a panel open: consume interact and jump (585-586, redundant because endFrame clears them). With no map open: prologue.update(dt, input) (588), then talkOrPickUp() takes the E press if there is something near on foot, otherwise player.update(sdt, input, rig.yaw, rig.pitch) (589-591).<br>7. rig.update(dt, input, player) (593), or rig.update(0.0001, ...) when paused (595).<br>8. Always: player.focus, then atmo.update (sky dome and shadow frustum) (598-599); world.cull (600); world uTime += dt; the Demer emissive pulse plus night; the oak leaves bob (601-605).<br>9. Always: px, py, pz = hero position on foot or the dragon (607-609). Toggle the #ui classes ui-open, st-fly, st-combat and st-hurt (610-614; st-hurt calls sheet.derived()).<br>10. If running and not paused: quests.update(px,py,pz,walk), which may save (616); actors.update(sdt) (617); readables.update(sdt) (618); rings.update(sdt, pos, fly) (619); course transitions (620-623); the Klokkenronde HUD innerHTML (624-625); tutorial accumulators (627-631); tutorial.update(dt, ctx) (632-640); crowd.update(sdt, ...) (641-643); life.update(dt, sdt, ..., fly, prologue.active) (644); the guide and hud quest target from quests.target() or nearestGiver (646-659); tracker list plus the Klokkenronde invitation (660-668); life.pointer override (669-673); prologue override (674-677); hud.trackerList (678); pop one toast every 1.9 s (679-682); the bells_ring edge and the periodic chime every 75 s (684-696); the discovery proximity loop, then kroniek.unlock, hud.discover, chime and 15 XP (697-707); map marks every 0.5 s (708-720).<br>11. Always: hud.setLocation(placeName or pace) (724-726); nearby() and hud.setPrompt priority (727-739); the action bar slots, then frame.update (742-756); banner.update(dt); hud.hold and guide.hush (757-759); guide.update(dt) (760); sound.wind (761); hud.update(dt) (762); post.render(dt) (763); input.endFrame() (764).<br>12. Adaptive resolution, if active and not paused: every 1.5 s of clamped dt, if fps &lt; 38 then ratio \*= 0.86 (minimum 0.55); if fps &gt; 57 then ratio \*= 1.07 (maximum maxRatio); then resize() reallocates every render target (766-778).<br><br>**world: Per-frame world upkeep**<br>1. main.ts:599 player.focus(focus)<br>2. main.ts:600 atmo.update: the dome follows the camera; shadow ortho extent clamp(110 + 2.4\*camY, 110, 760), resized when it changes by more than 4%; target snapped to whole shadow texels; bias and normalBias scaled by texel size (sky.ts:201-227)<br>3. main.ts:601 world.cull: per cullable, horizontal distance from the camera to its bounding sphere &lt; cullDist -&gt; visible (world.ts:70-79)<br>4. main.ts:602-606 uTime += dt (water ripples); Demer emissive = 0.3 + 0.12\*sin(0.7t) + 0.7\*night; each Root-Oak mote gets y += sin(...)\*0.012 per frame<br>5. main.ts:764 post.render (N8AO, bloom, grade) | core, world |  |
| 8 | Pointer lock, pause and resume | **core: Pointer lock, pause and resume**<br>1. requestLock at boot (435) and on every canvas click while no panel is open (496-498), via Input.requestLock (input.ts:113-123).<br>2. A failure before any success sets lockSupported=false for the session (input.ts:104-106, 117-122). From then on the mouse uses drag-to-look (input.ts:78-86) and the game never pauses.<br>3. Esc (the browser exits lock) fires pointerlockchange: paused = unlocked && kbm && lockSupported && no panel open (main.ts:499-505); hud.pauseOverlay shows GEPAUZEERD (hud.ts:118-123).<br>4. A click on the overlay re-locks; pointerlockchange then unpauses (506).<br>5. While paused, gameplay updates stop; rendering, the HUD, the banner, the guide and wall-clock setTimeouts keep running. Gamepad users never pause.<br><br>**player: Pointer lock and pause**<br>1. Boot main.ts:435 requestLock(). Canvas click main.ts:496-498 requests lock again unless a panel is open.<br>2. Esc (browser) -&gt; pointerlockchange -&gt; paused if on kbm, lock supported and no panel open (main.ts:499-507) -&gt; hud.pauseOverlay; an overlay click relocks.<br>4. On lock failure before any success, lockSupported=false for good (input.ts:104-106, 117-122) -&gt; drag-to-look: mousedown on the canvas sets dragging (85), and the same press also fires 'attack' (88).<br><br>**ui: Pause**<br>1. Mouse player presses Esc or alt-tabs: pointerlockchange (main.ts:500), paused if kbm, lock supported and no panel open.<br>2. hud.pauseOverlay(true) shows 'PAUZE · KLIK OM VERDER TE SPELEN' (hud.ts:118-123).<br>3. Click: input.requestLock(); on relock the overlay hides. The world, prologue and tutorial stop; banners, cards, compass and wind sound do not. | core, player, ui |  |
