### 3.3 Flight and movement

| # | Loop | Steps | Source maps | Tested |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 16 | Movement modes and transitions (reference) | 1. Modes (player.ts:11): fly, landing, walk, mounting, calling. Initial value 'fly' (35), with the hero seated (75-77).<br>2. fly -&gt; landing: E (or F, or pad Y) while canLand(): mode fly and altitude() &lt; 30 m (99-101, 114) -&gt; beginLanding (233-242).<br>3. landing -&gt; walk: after 1.1 s, updateLanding (244-262) -&gt; ev.land(0.6), trauma 0.22, dismount (264-279).<br>4. walk -&gt; mounting: E, not dead, distanceToDragon() &lt; 7 m (horizontal only, 103-105, 121-122), and only if talkOrPickUp found nothing nearby (main.ts:589) -&gt; beginMount (366-370).<br>5. mounting -&gt; fly: after 0.45 s, updateMount (372-380) -&gt; takeoff (382-396): speed 2, cruise 0, vy 9, ev.takeoff, trauma 0.3.<br>6. walk -&gt; calling: E with the dragon 7 m or more away (123) -&gt; beginCall (405-409).<br>7. calling -&gt; walk: after T = clamp(d/30, 1.6, 6) s, updateCall (411-443) -&gt; ev.land(0.5).<br>8. Scripted: summonFrom(x,y,z) (399-403) puts the dragon at a spot and starts calling (prologue.ts:345).<br>9. Teleports: debugPlaceOnFoot (471-489) from respawn (main.ts:858), prologue start (prologue.ts:158) and pose (main.ts:527); debugPlaceFlying (492-510) from prologue skip (main.ts:472) and pose (main.ts:529).<br>10. Sub-states that are derived, not named. On foot: idle / walk (heroSpeed 0.35-4.2) / run (over 4.2, Shift, max 6.8 m/s) / airborne (heroGrounded false) / locked (lock &gt; 0: attack, roll, prologue knock-down) / dead. In flight: hover (speed &lt; 6 flattens pitch; under 12, Space/C move straight up and down), cruise, low-capped near roofs (lowCap 175), boost (boostTime &gt; 0), ground skim (floor clamp 220-229), dive (energy 189).<br>11. Missing on purpose or not: you cannot dismount above 30 m, cancel a call, or interrupt landing or mounting; there is no crash state and no fall from the dragon; death in flight does not change flight. | player |  |
| 17 | Fly: steer with the mouse | 1. The mouse moves under pointer lock (or a drag), and input.ts:78-83 accumulates mouseDX/DY.<br>2. main.ts:550 input.poll(); then rig.update (rig.ts:48-49) turns rig.yaw/pitch by mouseDX\*0.0022 and clamps pitch to -1.35..1.2 (64).<br>3. Next frame, main.ts:591 player.update(sdt, input, rig.yaw, rig.pitch) -&gt; updateFlight (player.ts:138).<br>4. On keyboard and mouse: targetYaw = aimYaw - moveX\*0.35 (A/D only bias the heading by about 20 degrees, 150) and targetPitch = clamp(aimPitch, -1.2, 1.1) (151); Space forces pitch &gt;= 0.55 and C/Ctrl forces &lt;= -0.6 (153-154); near hover, pitch is pulled to 0 (156-157).<br>5. Yaw rate is limited by speed, 2.4 to 1.25 rad/s (160-165); pitch rate is clamped to 1.6 rad/s (166-167); roll banks from turnRate (204-205).<br>6. The dragon integrates along forward (208-212).<br>7. syncVisuals (446-460) sets dragon.root yaw and body pitch/roll, then dragon.update (dragon.ts:272-327) sets wingbeat, neck lead, tail lag and poseRig. The hero plays ride_idle with cloak wind (hero.ts:263-278).<br>8. rig.update: the focus leads along forward (rig.ts:75-77), distance is 9.5 + speed\*0.08 (79-80), camera roll follows the bank (111-112), and FOV widens from 60 to 84 with speed (124-127). Then post.render. | player |  |
| 18 | Fly: steer with a gamepad | 1. poll (input.ts:171-186): the left stick gives moveX/moveY (deadzone 0.15) and the right stick lookX/lookY. Device becomes 'pad' when any axis or button is active (177-178).<br>2. updateFlight: targetYaw = yaw - moveX\*1.6 and targetPitch = pitch + moveY\*0.9 (player.ts:146-148). Stick forward means nose UP, which contradicts the comment at input.ts:61.<br>3. rig.update: the right stick turns the camera (rig.ts:51-52). With the stick idle in fly mode, the camera auto-follows behind the dragon's yaw and pitch (53-62). | player |  |
| 19 | Fly: speed (throttle, wheel, boost, hover) | 1. W or ArrowUp (throttle=1), S or ArrowDown (brakeAxis=1) (input.ts:168-169); pad RT/LT as analog (184-185).<br>2. updateFlight: cruise += (throttle\*16 - brake\*30)\*dt + consumeWheel()\*4, clamped 0..52 (player.ts:171-172).<br>3. Low over the roofs, target = min(cruise, 8 + alt\*0.65) (174-176).<br>4. Shift, or pad X/LB/L3 (input.ts:137), with speed &gt; 8: boostTime = 1.4, target 82; trauma 0.32 only on a fresh press (177-186). Holding it re-triggers forever.<br>5. Energy: diving gains and climbing spends, scaled down near the ground (188-189); the approach rate is asymmetric (191-193).<br>6. Hover: below 12 m/s, Space/C set vy +-10 (196-201).<br>7. Feedback: dragon wingbeat rate and amplitude (dragon.ts:278-283), flap sound on the downstroke (287 -&gt; player.ts:74 -&gt; sound.flap), wind sound (main.ts:761), pace text (main.ts:726, 891-898), FOV and shake. | player |  |
| 20 | Fly and walk against the world: terrain assists and collision | **player: Fly: terrain assists and collisions**<br>1. Look-ahead: up to 30 m ahead, if the dragon is below the roof + clearance + 2 and pitch &lt; 0.35, pitch is nudged toward 0.35 (player.ts:215-219).<br>2. Hard floor: pos.y is clamped to heightAround(pos, 2.2) + 1.7. Impact &gt; 6 m/s gives trauma and costs 15% speed (220-229). Flying into a wall snaps the dragon onto the roof.<br>3. Ceiling: pos.y capped at 650 (230). There is no horizontal world limit.<br><br>**world: Walk and fly against the world (collision)**<br>1. Player input -&gt; player.update (main.ts:584)<br>2. player.ts:216-240 in flight: heightAround(ahead, 3) and heightAround(pos, 2.2) + CLEARANCE keep the dragon above roofs, columns and walls<br>3. On foot, player.ts:271-344: heightAround(next, R) decides blocked vs step-up; roofs are walkable (roofHeight follows the gable slope); the wall top is a flat 8.5 m<br>4. Landing and calling the dragon, player.ts:420-481: heightAround(.., 2) + 0.05<br>5. rig.ts:94-100: heightAt keeps the camera above surfaces and pulls it in when occluded<br>6. ### 3.4 Interaction, conversation and shops

| # | Loop | Steps | Source maps | Tested |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 26 | Interact on foot: E routing and the context prompt | **core: Interact (E, F or pad Y) on foot**<br>1. frameLoop: no prologue, walk mode, not dead, 250 ms since a panel closed, then talkOrPickUp() (589).<br>2. input.peek('interact'), then nearby() in priority order: actors.nearestNpc, combat.nearestLoot, actors.nearestPickup, readables.nearest, life.nearest (781-794).<br>3. Readable: kroniek.unlock; if new, +20+5\*level XP and a chime; then kroniekUI.show and life.onRead (802-813).<br>4. NPC: quests.conversationFor; kroniek.unlock('mens:id'); close the map; dialogue.start. On close: closeUi() and, if a quest event set pendingShop, chars.toggle(true, true, life.shopFor(id)) (814-826).<br>5. Life spot (merchant, board, door, well, shrine, person): close the map, then life.interact (827-829).<br>6. Loot: loot.take(). Quest pickup: quests.take and a chime (830-835).<br>7. Nothing near: the press is left for player.update, which lands the dragon, mounts it or calls it. The prompt text comes from the same nearby() call at 728-738.<br><br>**player: E routing on foot (talk, pick up, read vs dragon)**<br>1. E down -&gt; Input pressed 'interact' (input.ts:73).<br>2. main.ts:589: in walk mode, not dead, no prologue, and more than 250 ms after a panel closed -&gt; talkOrPickUp() (797-837) peeks interact.<br>3. nearby() (781-794) checks in priority order: named NPC, loot, quest pickup, readable, life spot. A hit consumes the press and opens dialogue / reads / picks up.<br>4. Nothing near -&gt; player.update -&gt; consume -&gt; mount (under 7 m) or call. Prompt text main.ts:732-738 ('Spreek met', 'Raap op', 'Lees', 'Bestijg', 'Roep').<br>5. Within 250 ms of a panel closing, talkOrPickUp is skipped but player.update still consumes E, so it mounts or calls the dragon (problem 1).<br>6. When talkOrPickUp returns true, player.update is skipped for that frame (591), so the hero's movement and animation miss one frame.<br><br>**ui: Context prompt**<br>1. ### 3.6 Combat

| # | Loop | Steps | Source maps | Tested |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 47 | Sword combo on foot | **combat: Sword combo (LMB on foot)**<br>1. LMB sets Input 'attack' (input.ts:88; pad RT input.ts:200).<br>2. main.ts:582 calls Combat.update, then footActions (combat.ts:352). It is skipped while rolling (370-379 eats the press).<br>3. With no attack running, startAttack(COMBO[comboT&gt;0 ? combo : 0]) runs (384, 439). assist() (420) picks the best enemy within 6 m and ±1.4 rad of the stick or camera direction (score = dist + 3·angle) and snaps heroYaw to it. Lunge = clamp(dist - radius - 1, 0, max(def.lunge, 2.6)). hero.act(clip, dur) and sound.swing(heavy) play; comboT = dur + 0.55.<br>4. Each frame: atk.t += dt, player.lock = 0.05, player.push() covers the lunge spread over the windup (399-402).<br>5. At atk.t &gt;= def.hit, resolveSwing runs (455). fx.slash arc; every live enemy within def.range + its radius, \|dy\| &lt; 3 and inside ±arc/2 takes damage(e, rollDamage(d, mult), heavy, fwd).<br>6. damage (699): hp -= dmg; white flash; aggro; resonance +8 (+12 heavy); a floater with the number ('dmg' or 'dmg crit'); fx.sparks; sound.hit; tally.hits++; camp-mates within 20 m aggro. Then kill(), or poiseHits++ and either stagger (heavy, or poise reached) or a 2.5 knockback nudge; an elite becomes bossTarget.<br>7. If anything was hit: hitstop = def.stop (+0.02 when several are hit), which sets timeScale 0.05 next frame (270-272), and ev.trauma 0.16 / 0.32 heavy goes to the camera shake.<br>8. Pressing LMB after 35 % of dur sets queued. At dur end the next COMBO step starts (407-411). Pressing earlier is consumed and lost. After dur + 0.55 s without a press the combo resets to blow 1.<br><br>**player: Fight on foot: how combat drives the player**<br>1. LMB / pad RT -&gt; 'attack' pressed (input.ts:88, 199-200) -&gt; combat.footActions (combat.ts:380-383) -&gt; startAttack -&gt; assist() picks a target from moveDir/camYaw and sets p.heroYaw (419-436) -&gt; hero.act('attack1\|2\|3') (449).<br>2. Every attack frame: p.lock = 0.05 (396), so Player.updateWalk zeroes the target speed and turning (player.ts:308-310); the lunge goes through p.push (combat.ts:398-401 -&gt; player.ts:283-296).<br>3. Space/pad A in combat -&gt; combat peeks and consumes 'jump' before Player sees it (combat.ts:355-367) -&gt; roll: invulnerable 0.42 s, heroYaw set, hero.act('roll', 0.55), push for 0.55 s (369-377).<br>4. RMB/LT -&gt; skill, R/pad B -&gt; skill2 (386-391) -&gt; hero.act('cast') etc.<br>5. Hit taken -&gt; hero.act('hit', 0.35) (1085); trauma -&gt; rig.addTrauma; hitstop -&gt; combat.timeScale scales sdt for player.update (main.ts:582).<br>6. setArmed shows the sword in a fight or for 8 s after (combat.ts:325 -&gt; hero.ts:210-213). | combat, player |  |

### 3.7 Town life

| # | Loop | Steps | Source maps | Tested |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 65 | Street crowd: placing and recycling walkers | 1. Boot: Crowd constructor (crowd.ts:72-181) samples every OSM road of a kept kind near Diest or within 420 m of Scherpenheuvel (-5062,518) every metre, cuts it where it runs into a house (collision heightAround &gt; 0.05), stores a side offset per point and links line ends (addLine/key, :183-201). It builds 36 walkers with kit.build(r), or with the stopgap dress(pickRole) path when there is no kit.<br>2. Every frame crowd.update (:311): when altitude &lt; 140, every 0.25 s the first walker with no line or post, or more than 95 m away, is placed (place(), :271). A soldier tries a gate post first (60%, postAt :238, at most 2 per gate, 9-70 m from the focus). Otherwise a role anchor (ANCHORS :508: beguine at the Begijnhof, pilgrim at the basilica, friar at Sint-Sulpitius, market woman, burgher) or any line. The spot must be 9-70 m from the focus and, in the first 8 tries, outside the camera view.<br>3. A walker moves along its line at its pace, keeps to its side (:375-379), takes another line at a corner (80%) or turns back, sometimes stands, steps round the player within 1.2 m, and faces the player when standing within 6 m.<br>4. Animation: walk, run, talk and idle weights blend; the walk timeScale follows the stride so feet do not slide; mixers update at 1/2/4 frame intervals by distance (:395-415).<br>5. The player sees walkers appear on streets around them and vanish beyond 95 m; nobody shows when flying higher than 140 m. | life |  |
| 66 | Walker barks | **life: Walker barks**<br>1. On foot within 3.6 m of a walker whose bark cooldown has run out, with the global cooldown out too: a 60% chance per frame (crowd.ts:417).<br>2. say() (:466) picks the walker's role line (folk.ts LINES, 45%) or line() (:474): GREET, plus FAMOUS when renown for Diest + Begijnhof &gt;= 15, BELLS while main_bells is active or not yet started, and lines for flags and quests (seals, toll, roelof, kludde, level 5).<br>3. quests.fill fills {name}, {dragon} and {heer\|juffrouw}; the bark callback adds combat.floaters 'bark' over the head; the walker plays the talk clip for 2.6 s; cooldowns are 25 s per walker and 3.5 s overall.<br><br>**content: Crowd barks**<br>1. On foot within 3.6 m of a walker, with no global bark in the last 3.5 s, at a 60 % roll (crowd.ts:417).<br>2. 45 % of the time a role line from LINES, otherwise line(): GREET, FAMOUS when renown Diest+Begijnhof is at least 15, BELLS while main_bells is active or not started, and quest-flag lines (474-488).<br>3. The text is filled and floats over the head (crowd.ts:466-471, main.ts:367). When the dragon lands within 16 m, a FLEE bark (349). | life, content |  |
| 67 | The dragon lands among people | 1. player.mode walk or calling sets dragonDown in main.ts:643.<br>2. In crowd.update (:342-350), a walker within 16 m of the dragon flees for 4 s at 2.4x speed, away from the dragon along its line, and sometimes shouts a FLEE line.<br>3. Gate soldiers on posts turn to face the dragon within 30 m and do not flee (:337).<br>4. Fleeing walkers cannot be spoken to (nearestWalker skips flee &gt; 0, :433). | life |  |
| 68 | Talk to a walker (main menu) | **life: Talk to a walker (main menu)**<br>1. Walk within 2.6 m of a walker with no merchant, board or readable nearer. main.ts nearby() -&gt; life.nearest (life.ts:630-646): services first (3.2-3.8 m), then the board (3.2 m), then crowd.nearestWalker, then spots.<br>2. The prompt reads 'E Spreek de/het &lt;label&gt; aan'; persona() (:746) derives the name and label from the crowd index, the role and the body sex (seeded, stable for the session).<br>3. Press E: interact -&gt; talkWalker (:769). crowd.engage(w, 9999) stops the walker and turns it to the player; talk.speaker('life_walker', name, label); talk.start shows the greeting greetFor(role faction) by standing tier (TIER_GREET).<br>4. Choices: the role's ask (ROLE_INFO[role].ask -&gt; special()), 'Waar ben ik?' (where), 'Wat valt er hier te doen?' (todo), 'Vertel eens iets.' (gossip), BYE (closes with the toast 'NAAM · BYE_REPLY').<br>5. Each answer comes through after(): 'MORE' returns to menuBack, BYE closes. On close, Talk's closed callback sets walker.chat = 3 (it lingers, then walks on) and calls closeUi().<br><br>**content: Talk to any street walker**<br>1. crowd.nearestWalker plus E leads to life.interact. Name from the NAMES pools (life.ts:749-760), greeting from TIER_GREET[faction][tier] (766).<br>2. Menu: where am I (WHERE_FRAMES + PLACE_LINES for the nearest discovery, which unlocks plaats:\*, life.ts:819-829); what to do (TODO_OPEN + TODO[kind] with {dir} and {far}, 874-877); tell me something (GOSSIP[role] + REACTIVE gated by cond(), 909, 881-905); the role special from ROLE_INFO[role].ask (917).<br>3. Specials: work (WORK), food (FOOD + FOOD_GOODS shop), town (TOWN_VIEW), rep (REP + REP_DEEDS), tend (TEND, 2 stuivers), camps (CAMPS_WARN, marks the map), bless (BLESS, zegen buff 300 s), pilgrim (PILGRIM_TALES, points to the basilica), dare (DARES race or guess), tale (TALES, unlocks a Kroniek page through TALE_PAGES) (life.ts:928-1021).<br>4. Close with MORE, BYE and BYE_REPLY. | life, content |  |
| 69 | Walker: 'Waar ben ik?' | 1. where() (life.ts:815) finds the nearest discovery that has PLACE_LINES, within max(160, 2.2r). Otherwise it uses Diest when inside the walls, otherwise a 'nowhere' line.<br>2. It frames the line with WHERE_FRAMES[role] plus any, and adds the direction and distance when more than 70 m away.<br>3. The first time: kroniek.unlock('plaats:&lt;key&gt;'), discovery.found = true, the toast 'KRONIEK · &lt;title&gt;', +15 XP. | life |  |

(the pointer) | 1. todo() (life.ts:869) -&gt; todoTarget (:841) scores unread readables, unmet services, the board (if there are open jobs), uncleared camps up to hero level + 2, quest givers with '!', unfound discoveries, unread signs and unvisited shrines, by distance x weight, skipping anything within 7 m.<br>2. LinePicker.pick prefers lines not heard in the last 10 minutes of game time; quests.fill fills the names. | life |  |

Shop: shopFor builds the view from GOODS, GEAR_STOCK, SPECIALS and TALISMANS. Prices fall with standing; tiered goods are locked (life.ts:1273-1278, shop.ts). E within 3.2 m -&gt; talkBoard (life.ts:1238) -&gt; Board.todays() (board.ts:58): 3 jobs (4 from level 3) built from job\_&lt;date&gt;\_&lt;slot&gt;; the slot sets the kind by the date's parity (bounty, delivery, read, fetch).<br>3. Bounty: kill N enemies at the camp (a kill objective) -&gt; stage pay -&gt; the client shows '?' -&gt; talk to the client: 'Het werk is gedaan…' -&gt; apply done and complete -&gt; quest reward + Life.completed: +5 standing with the client's faction.<br>5. Delivery: talk to the sender: 'Ik kom &lt;what&gt; halen.' (the item name is parsed back out of the objective text, life.ts:1083) -&gt; give item, stage bring -&gt; talk to the client: 'Hier is je bestelling.' -&gt; take the item, complete -&gt; +4 standing.<br>6. Read: read 3 distinct readables (main.ts:813 -&gt; onRead, life.ts:707) -&gt; toast 'OPDRACHT · n van 3 gelezen' -&gt; done read -&gt; pay -&gt; client: 'Ik heb gelezen…' -&gt; complete -&gt; +4.<br>7. Fetch: pick up the spawned items at the FETCH_SPOTS location -&gt; pay -&gt; client: 'Ik heb je spullen gevonden.' -&gt; take the items, complete -&gt; +4.<br>8. The next real day brings new jobs; jobs taken earlier are rebuilt from their id and pin (Board constructor, board.ts:52-55).<br><br>**rpg: Notice-board job as an engine quest**<br>1. The board (Life.talkBoard) lists Board.todays(): 3 jobs, 4 from level 3, seeded by date.<br>2. 'Ik neem het aan.': Board.pinLevel writes flags job\_&lt;id&gt;\_lv and \_pin, then Board.take calls q.start(id) directly. Banner, tracked = the job.<br>3. Bounty: kill objective on a camp, then stage pay. Delivery: talk 'pick' (the service hand-over gives the item, done, stage bring), then talk 'bring'. Read: a flag objective completed by life.onRead. Fetch: collect spawns.<br>4. Hand-over at the client's service dialogue (life.ts:1078-1097): q.apply([take item, done, complete]). Reward, then Life.completed adds standing.<br><br>**content: Notice board jobs**<br>1. E at the aanplakbord (-33,87). board.ts rolls the day's jobs from JOBS templates, clients and FETCH_SPOTS.<br>2. Take a job: a quest job\_\* with its notice and journal lines. Do it (bounty, delivery, read N readables, fetch 3 things). Return to the client for the thanks line and the pay.<br>3. Three jobs done turns on REACTIVE 'many_jobs'. | life, rpg, content |  |

A door gives a DOOR_LINE once a real day (a heal of 20% if the line mentions apple or porridge), XP the first time, and a 'nobody opens' line on repeat. Well: WELL_LINES and a heal, which unlocks tijd:putten (1385-1387).<br>3. Shrine: SHRINE_LINES plus a once-a-day blessing, which unlocks tijd:kapelletjes (1392-1399).<br>4. Standing.add: a gain of 3 or more costs the rival max(1, round(n/4)) (kroon↔oranje, kerk↔vrije). Points are clamped at 0 or above. ### 3.8 World, exploration and the daily loop

| # | Loop | Steps | Source maps | Tested |

| 86 | Discover a place | **world: Discover a place**<br>1. Fly or walk near a POI or named building that has a LORE entry<br>2. main.ts:698-708 each frame (skipped while the prologue runs): radius = d.r + (flying ? min(alt\*0.5, 120) : 0)<br>3. Inside: d.found = true; kroniek.unlock('plaats:'+key) (saved; the Kroniek toast is suppressed for 'plaats', main.ts:239)<br>4. hud.discover(d) (hud.ts:137) queues a card: kicker 'ONTDEKT' (major) or 'GEZIEN', then title and text<br>5. sound.chime (strong for Sint-Sulpitius before the bells ring); quests.addXp(15)<br>6. Afterwards: the map shows the found title (major unfound ones as '?'); the Kroniek 'Plaatsen' page lists the card<br><br>**core: Discovering a place**<br>1. For each undiscovered world.discoveries entry (not during the prologue): the radius is d.r, plus min(py\*0.5, 120) when flying (697-700).<br>2. Inside it: d.found=true; kroniek.unlock('plaats:key'); hud.discover(d) card; chime (strong for Sint-Sulpitius before the bells ring); +15 XP (701-706).<br>3. The Kroniek unlock callback skips the toast for 'plaats' entries (238).<br><br>**ui: Discovery**<br>1. Within d.r (plus up to 120 m when flying) of an unfound discovery (main.ts:698-708): found, kroniek.unlock('plaats:key'), hud.discover, sound.chime (cracked for Sint-Sulpitius until the bells ring), +15 xp.<br>2. Hud.update (hud.ts:158-165) shows the card 'ONTDEKT'/'GEZIEN' · title · text for 7.5 s, 1.2 s gap, held while a banner is active.<br>3. The compass mark turns ◆ (found) and the map shows the name.<br><br>**content: Discovery cards**<br>1. Fly or walk within the discovery radius (main.ts:699-702; in flight the radius grows with height).<br>2. kroniek.unlock('plaats:key') (place pages give no toast), hud.discover queues the card 'ONTDEKT' (major) or 'GEZIEN' (hud.ts:137-165), with a chime and 15 XP. | world, core, ui, content |  |

## 4. Content

The playable chapter is Diest and Scherpenheuvel in 1650. All player-facing text is Dutch (Flemish, je-form), about 28,800 words: 25,898 in the eight data sources and about 2,750 inside code files.

### 4.1 Inventory

Counts were reconciled against the code where maps differed (section 2.6).

| What | Count | Where | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| Quests written in content | 10 | `src/content/diest.ts` | 4 main, 5 side, 1 contract. 27 stages, 27 objectives: 16 talk, 5 kill, 3 collect, 2 reach, 1 flag (the flag can never be met). |
| Quests made at runtime | 2 kinds | `src/life/life.ts`, `src/life/board.ts` | The town walk `walk_diest` (8 stops) and notice-board jobs (3 a day, 4 from level 3, from 5 templates: 2 bounty, 1 delivery, 1 read, 1 fetch). |
| Branching quests | 2 | `diest.ts` | `side_seals` (reported, begijnhof, warned) and `side_toll` (paid, bribed, seized). |
| Dialogue nodes | 100 | `diest.ts` | 37 choices, 69 effects, 2,632 words. No missing or unreachable node. |
| Named quest characters | 11 | `diest.ts` | 7 of them give quests. Listed in 4.2. |
| Named merchants, guides and gatekeepers | 20 | `src/content/diest_life.ts` | 18 in Diest, 2 in Scherpenheuvel; 10 have a shop. |
| Market-day hawkers | 8 | `life.ts:105` | 3 calls each, held in code. |
| Street crowd | 36 walkers | `src/rpg/crowd.ts` | 11 roles, 90 names, 18 kit archetypes. |
| Items | 10 | `diest.ts` | 7 marked as quest items. Food items are added at runtime. |
| Enemy types | 5 | `src/combat/enemies.ts` | deserteur, musketier, broekhond, dwaallicht, kludde. |
| Camps | 7 | `diest.ts` | 27 enemies in total, 2 named elites (De Kludde, Lambrecht de Rode). |
| Origins (classes) and skills | 3 and 6 | `src/rpg/character.ts` | Schutter, Beiaardier (id `klokkenluider`), Smokkelaar; the second skill unlocks at level 3. |
| Backgrounds | 5 | `character.ts` | diest, kempen, hageland, luik, tros; one perk each. |
| Factions and tiers | 5 and 5 | `src/rpg/factions.ts` | gilden, kerk, kroon, oranje, vrije; tiers at 0, 10, 25, 50, 90 points. |
| Gear | 8 uniques | `character.ts` | Plus 14 random bases, 7 suffixes, 12 rare names, 4 rarities. |
| Goods for sale | 51 | `diest_life.ts` | Across 10 sellers, plus 3 foods from walkers, 6 talismans, 4 specials. |
| Kroniek entries | 83 | `src/rpg/kroniek.ts` (64), `diest_life.ts` PAGES (19) | Places 29, tijd en zeden 32, mensen 12, wezens 10. Truth labels: 57 geschiedenis, 7 volksverhaal, 19 verzonnen. |

### 4.2 Cast

**Quest characters (11, in `diest.ts`).**

| Id | Name | Role and place | Wants |
|---|---|---|---|
| `pieter` | Pieter Vandenbempt | Koster, by Sint-Sulpitius (30, 55) | The clappers back, and no questions from the chapter's dean. |
| `marieke` | Marieke Smets | Marskramer (shop), Grote Markt (55, 95) | To sell; watches for the girl for free. |
| `joos` | Meester Joos Peeters | Deken van de wevers, Lakenhalle (75, 45) | The forger found and the seal's name kept. |
| `aleydis` | Zuster Margriet | Begijn en ziekenverzorgster, Begijnhof (680, -316) | Herbs for the fever, shelter for widows. |
| `drossaard` | Jonker Hendrik van Gorp | Drossaard, 400 m east (400, 140) | His letter carried unread and the law kept, with an empty treasury. |
| `wouter` | Wouter Claes | Tollenaar, Putterstraatpoort (-95, 340) | The smuggler caught and his share of the fine. |

| `gielis` | Gielis "de Ton" | Voerman, outside the Allerheiligenpoort (-430, 100) | To dodge the toll. |
| `driesen` | Boer Jan Driesen | Farmer, Kaggevinne (-2235, 690) | Lambrecht dead and his pigs back. |
| `truyken` | Truyken | Seller, Scherpenheuvel church (-5005, 545) | To sell, and to be rid of the humming figure. |
| `provost` | Pater Jan Cools | Overste, Scherpenheuvel (-5085, 470) | The wisps gone; has an answer for the drossaard. |

**Merchants, guides and gatekeepers (20, in `diest_life.ts`).** On the market: bakker Jan Verhoeven, kruidenvrouw Wanne Coels, boekverkoper en drukker Lucas Haeck, kleermaker Cornelis Mertens, wapensmid Gillis Bosmans, omroeper Rombout Smolders. Around town: notaris Adriaan Wellens, waard Mattheus Ceulemans (De Zwaan), Zuster Machteld (infirmerie), Sergeant Alonso Ruiz (by the Beverenpoort), kastelein Daneel Vander Borcht (Hof van Nassau). Seven gatekeepers: Jaspar Jacobs (Schaluinpoort), Wouter Peeters (Schaffensepoort), Lenaert Vos (Koepoort), Andries Wouters (Beverenpoort), Jacob Smets (Putterstraatpoort), Hendrik Coeckx (Allerheiligenpoort), Pauwel Geens (Demer water gate). In Scherpenheuvel: Lysken Mols (pilgrims' tokens) and Heer Willem Pauwels (pilgrims' guide).

**Others.** Neelke, the hero's sister (prologue only). The two named elites, De Kludde (L5) and Lambrecht de Rode (L5). The dragon, named by the player (10 suggestions). Mentioned but not met: professor Fromondus, Jan Vranckx (Lijsbet's late husband), the pigs Pier, Pol and Mieke, the veerman and his missing boy, the mute boy from the Schaffensepoort, Zuster Clara, and historical figures (Filips Willem, Willem II and his son, Jan Berchmans, Albrecht and Isabella, Leopold Willem, Condé, Turenne, Mazarin, Cobergher). The street crowd draws from 90 names in 6 pools.

### 4.3 Inconsistencies between files

- **The hero's home.** The prologue walks the hero home to the walled-up Koepoort, and the Diest background says "born inside the walls" (`prologue.ts:69-71`, `character.ts:122`). The first quest is "Een vreemdeling in Diest", and the crowd says "Jij bent niet van hier, hé?" (`diest.ts:162`, `crowd.ts:495`). Nobody in town knows Neelke.
- **The calendar.** The silent night is "zaterdag op zondag" and the hero flies straight to Diest, but people speak of "drie dagen later" and "gisteren"; the crier already has news from 14 November 1650, and the chapter-3 plan dates the boy's stones to Allerheiligen, 1 November 1650 (a Tuesday) (`prologue.ts:65`, `diest.ts:490`, `diest_talk.ts:271`, `kroniek.ts:78`, `docs/design/chapters/3-leuven.md:21`).
- **The prophecy.** The prologue's key line promises that "all the bells of Brabant ringing together" ends it (`prologue.ts:59`); `story.md` puts the decision at Klokke Roeland in Ghent, with chapter 2 in Loon, outside Brabant, and three endings.
- **Gates.** Each gate has up to three names: legacy world keys (Antwerpsepoort, Maastrichtsepoort, Leuvensepoort in `lore.ts` and `tools/history/walls_1560.json`), the shown names (Schaluinpoort, Beverenpoort, Putterstraatpoort), and the `COCKS` spellings in `life.ts:127-128`. The Koepoort (walled up after 1508) and the "Demerpoort" (not a historic gate) both have guards and gate lore.
- **Places.** Scherpenheuvel is at (-5040, 560) for respawn but (-5062, 518) everywhere else. The crowd's market anchor is (0, 0) with radius 90 m while the market centre is (-38, 98) (`crowd.ts:512`, `diest_life.ts:17`), so market women gather at Sint-Sulpitius.
- **Characters described two ways.** Truyken sells pilgrims' tokens (`diest.ts:139`) or statuettes (Lysken: "Truyken verkoopt beeldjes. Ik verkoop tekens", `diest_life.ts:132`). The drossaard is in the oranje party (`diest_life.ts:147`) but talked about as the Crown's man (`diest_talk.ts:62`, 296, 410). Marieke is marskramer or marskraamster. The overste is still "proost" in an objective id, the glossary and `voice.md`. Two beguines run the sickroom (Margriet and Machteld).
- **Names that collide.** The beguine name pool contains "Zuster Machteld", the named infirmary sister. "Janneke" is in the women's and boys' pools. The gatekeeper Lenaert Vos shares a first name with the planned antagonist, Meester Lenaert the Klokkengieter; gatekeeper Jaspar Jacobs shares one with the planned artillery clerk, whose surname differs between the chapter-2 and chapter-3 plans.
- **Ids from before renames.** `aleydis` is Zuster Margriet, `side_roelof`, `wezen:roelof` and `roelofs_slagzwaard` are Lambrecht de Rode, `provost` is the overste, origin `klokkenluider` is shown as Beiaardier. These ids are save keys.
- **Money.** The save field and the quest effect are called `florins` but count stuivers (1 florijn = 20 stuivers, as the glossary says). The quest engine's toasts say "+30 florins" in English while everything else says stuivers.
- **Two reputations.** Quests still pay per-place renown (Diest, Begijnhof, Scherpenheuvel), shown in the journal as "Aanzien", while faction standing is also shown as "AANZIEN" in the character panel. The crowd measures fame by renown (Diest plus Begijnhof at least 15); town life measures it by faction points (at least 40).
- **Truth labels.** The notary's topic "eik" is labelled geschiedenis but opens a page labelled verzonnen; the topic "nacht" is verzonnen but opens a volksverhaal; the Tafelrondmotte card is history but labelled volksverhaal. "De Basiliek" is a title from 1922.
- **History corrections applied unevenly.** The Kludde's flames, "thrice round the church", the unfinished tower and the beeldenstorm are stated one way in the Kroniek and another way in street talk; the printer and the pilgrims' guide's title were not corrected (`docs/research/history-check.md` items A15, F8, F48, F69, F81, 1b).
- **Prices.** Holy water costs 6 stuivers for 10 minutes at the infirmary and 4 for 15 minutes at the tokens stall; the arms book is 9 at tier 2 at the printer and 8 at tier 0 at the castellan; the same map is 8 or 7; salve is 5 or 6. Six good ids are reused across shops with different names or prices. A bed costs 4 at De Zwaan while a pilgrim calls Scherpenheuvel's 12 "het dubbele".
- **Lijsbet and the children.** The Kroniek gives Lijsbet three children and the crowd says they eat at the neighbours, but she is built as an old grey-haired woman and never mentions them. The veerman's boy is still missing in one line and has come back in another.
- **Length limits.** Two Kroniek entries exceed `voice.md`'s 60 words, three discovery cards have four sentences (limit three), and eight fixed barks exceed 12 words.
- **Docs against the game.** `gameplay.md` still describes five slides, "gij", other origin names, 63 Kroniek entries and a three-act story that `story.md` replaced; `voice.md`'s model sentences still contain facts the history check corrected.

### 4.4 Content counts by subsystem

Every content count from the eight maps, as they reported them. Where a count was wrong, section 2.6 gives the checked number.

| Subsystem | What | Count | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| core | URL flags | 7 | lowq; q=low\|medium\|high (not validated: q=foo gives AO 'Low' with no sun shadow); dpr=&lt;float&gt; (turns the adaptive scaler off; NaN if malformed); autostart; world=&lt;name&gt; (public/world/&lt;name&gt;.json, default diest_1650); sky=&lt;preset&gt; (default golden); prologue. The README also documents ?t=0.92, which no longer exists. |

| core | Keyboard bindings | 25 | 17 KEYMAP entries (E and F both interact, Tab and I both character, Ctrl and C both descend, both Shifts boost) plus 8 movement keys (WASD and the arrows), read in poll(). |
| core | Gamepad bindings | 15 | 8 edge buttons (Y interact, A jump, Back map, RB journal, D-down potion, B skill2, Start help, D-up time), 2 triggers as presses (RT attack, LT skill), 5 held mappings (A jump, B descend, X/LB/L3 boost, Y interact, RT attack). Kroniek, character and paint have no pad button. |
| core | Quality tiers | 3 | high: N8AO Medium at full resolution, bloom, SMAA, 4096 shadow map, adaptive DPR up to 1.5. medium: N8AO Low at half resolution, 2048 shadows. low: no AO, bloom or SMAA, no shadows, DPR 0.8, 512 px texture layers, MSAA that has no effect. |
| core | Post passes | 4 | N8AOPass, UnrealBloomPass (0.2 strength, 0.6 radius, 1.15 threshold; strength rises with night), the final grade, SMAA. Final grade uniforms: exposure, shadow and highlight tint, saturation, contrast 0.18, vignette 0.32, grain 0.022. |
| core | Loading messages | 12 | 6 in main.ts (the initial line plus 5 setLoading calls) and 6 inside world.build, which never paint because the build is synchronous. |

| core | HUD prompt variants | 10 | prologue prompt, Spreek met (· winkel), Raap op (loot by rarity colour), Lees/Herlees, Raap op (quest item), life prompt, Landen, Bestijg &lt;dragon&gt;, Roep &lt;dragon&gt;, none. |

| core | Place-name rules | 6 | nearest discovery within min(1.6r, 260) m; inside the walls; within 220 m of a gate; 3 hard-coded villages (Scherpenheuvel, Kaggevinne, Webbekom); fields within 2000 m; otherwise Hertogdom Brabant. |

| combat | Feel numbers: player | 30 | Combo queue opens at 35 % of the swing; combo reset 0.55 s after a swing ends; assist reach 6 m, cone ±1.4 rad, score dist + 3·angle, lunge stops 1.0 m short, max lunge 2.6 m. Roll 0.55 s, speed 11·(1-k)^0.7 (\~3.56 m), invuln 0.42 s, cannot start before contact + 0.05 s, only while inCombat. Hit-stop timescale 0.05; stops: light 0.06, heavy 0.12/0.13, +0.02 multi-hit, kill 0.15, elite kill 0.28, explosion 0.06, tone 0.07, klokslag 0.10. Crit ×1.75 (base 5 % + 0.6/list, max 60 %); buff ×1.33. Potion 50 % over 1.25 s, cd 12 s. Out-of-combat regen 4 %/s; resonance +8 hit, +12 heavy, -5/s out of combat; inCombat = 5 s since the last fight or an aggro enemy within 30 m; sword drawn 8 s. Death wait 3.4 s. Incoming damage ×100/(100 + 2.2·armor), min 1. No i-frames or stagger after being hit. |

Rig: shake = trauma², max 0.06 rad rotation, decay 0.9/s, noise 18 Hz (rig.ts:114-121). |

Stagger 0.42 s for everyone; knockback nudge 2.5, poise break 4, heavy 7, kill 6; elites ÷2. White-flash decay 7/s; red telegraph ramps over windup, decays 5/s. Enemy damage ×0.85-1.15; cooldown ×0.7-1.3; first cd 0-1 s. Turn rate 8 chase, 5 melee windup, 3 ranged windup, 3 wander. Musket aim lag 1.5/s, hit radius 1.4 m (3.5 m flying). Orb 9 m/s, homing 0.9/s, life 5 s, hit radius 1.1 m (3 m). Radius 0.45 (human/wisp), 0.6×scale (hound). Separation radius sum + 0.4 at 8×overlap. Circle ring 4.8 m (4 m vs flyer) at 0.45 speed. Tokens 2 (+1 ranged). |

## 5. Structural problems

All problems from the eight maps, merged where they are the same problem and ranked by how much each holds back the quality of the game: first what every player meets and what breaks progress, then what weakens the core feel (flight, combat, guidance), then content and story consistency, then structure that slows every later fix, then cost and clean-up. Kind is the mappers' label (fragile, unfinished, inconsistent, tangled, slow, duplicate, unclear, dead). Size is the largest estimate among the merged problems. The last column names the job that is rewriting the files involved right now; a fix there must be coordinated with that job or wait for its merge.

| Rank | Problem | Kind | Where | What to do | Size | Being rewritten now |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **Closing a conversation or reading card with E calls or mounts the dragon (Space makes the hero jump or roll).** Input registers 'interact' on the same keydown that the dialogue uses to close itself. _(maps: player)_ | fragile | `main.ts:589-591, 839-842`; `input.ts:67-73`; `rpgui.ts:33-35, 91-100`; `kroniekui.ts:32-35` | In closeUi(), drop the pending interact and jump (input.consume both), or have Input clear pressed when a panel closes. Make the guard block the press, not reroute it. | small | — |
| 2 | **Reloading during the prologue leaves a save with no main quest, no prologue and no starting weapon.** A new game is written once at main.ts:164 with created = true and empty gear. _(maps: rpg, ui)_ | fragile | `main.ts:159-170, 228-233, 340, 441-443, 468-483` / `main.ts:158`; `prologue.ts` | Build the whole new-game state (hero, gear, hp, standing) before the one writeSave. Decide the opening from the save, not from the front-end result: if the save is created but has neither flags.prologue nor quests.main_arrive, run (or offer to skip) the prologue; on skip or finish always call beginChapter. | small | opening |
| 3 | **Input swallows Space, Tab and arrows in the name fields, and replays typed letters as game actions.** Every new player meets this at creation. _(maps: core)_ | fragile | `core/input.ts:68-75`; `main.ts:67`; `ui/creation.ts:188-190` | In the keydown handler, return early when e.target is an input, textarea, select or contenteditable element, and never preventDefault there. Add Input.reset(), which clears pressed, keys and mouse, and call it once when the game takes over (after await front). | small | character kit |
| 4 | **The boot pointer-lock request can turn pointer lock off for the whole session; drag-to-look then also attacks.** Browsers refuse requestPointerLock without a recent user gesture. _(maps: core, player)_ | fragile | `main.ts:435, 504`; `core/input.ts:104-106, 117-122` / `main.ts:435`; `input.ts:102-106, 113-123` | Do not treat a lock attempt made without a user gesture as proof that lock is unsupported: drop the request at boot or only conclude 'unsupported' inside a sandboxed iframe or on a SecurityError. Retry on every canvas click. In drag mode, count a press as an attack only if the mouse barely moved. Clear the mouse set on blur. Show a 'klik om te sturen' hint until the first lock. | small | opening (start-of-game flow in main.ts) |
| 5 | **Front-end handover shows a black, frozen screen, and the synchronous world build freezes creation.** This is the first impression of every session. _(maps: core, world)_ | slow | `main.ts:153-155` / `world.ts:81-95, 294-400`; `crowd.ts:79-103` | Keep the curtain (or the front end's last screen) up until the first real frame has rendered. Make world.build async with a frame between stages so creation stays alive and the progress lines paint. Compile shaders behind the curtain (renderer.compileAsync) and pre-warm enemy, crowd and prologue materials. Bucket the collision columns so tree placement and crowd sampling are cheaper. | medium | opening (start-of-game flow in main.ts) |
| 6 | **Gamepad is advertised but menus, dialogue and panels cannot be used with one; RT throttles and breathes fire at once.** In flight, every RT pull past half speeds up and also streams fire every 0.3 s. _(maps: player, ui)_ | unfinished | `input.ts:139, 175-185, 199-201`; `combat.ts:581`; `rpgui.ts:24-37`; `gamehud.ts:322`; `kroniekui.ts:31`; `share.ts:37`; `main.ts:744-755`; `prologue.ts:387`; `hud.ts:65` / `rpgui.ts:25-39, 71`; `main.ts:586`; `creation.ts`; `kroniekui.ts:31-36, 66`; `gamehud.ts:323`; `share.ts:37`; `core/input.ts:188-197`; `prologue.ts:119-124, 387`; `tutorial.ts:64-72` | Give breath its own button in flight. Route pad input into the open panel through Input actions (confirm, back, up, down) instead of raw keydown listeners, map the Kroniek and character panels to pad buttons, and draw every key glyph from one device-aware binding table (prologue, dialogue, reading card, slots, prompts). | large | opening, character kit, sixth faction |
| 7 | **Dragon teleports up walls: the hard floor snaps height in one frame; no wall response or crash.** floor = heightAround(pos, 2.2) + 1.7 jumps from 0 to roof height at a wall, so flying into a tower lifts the dragon 20 m in one frame. _(maps: player)_ | fragile | `player.ts:214-229` | Treat footprint walls as horizontal obstacles: sweep the move against heightAround at the new x,z, and if the step-up is more than a small amount, slide along the wall, cut speed and shake (a 'graze'), or bounce. Keep the vertical floor as a soft spring for roofs. Let the look-ahead add climb instead of setting pitch. | medium | — |
